## 252. [Commodifying Climate: Understanding Data Centres in Brazil](<http://hdl.handle.net/10393/52249>)

- 作者：Gustavo Soares Denani
- 发表日期：2026-11-19
- 引用次数：0
- DOI：https://doi.org/10.20381/ruor-32383
- [打开 PDF](<https://ruor.uottawa.ca/bitstreams/09e0e291-4b01-48c3-b518-3e965d2b8e8a/download>)

**摘要：**

Data centres have come under increasing scrutiny due to their demand for natural resources. As a symptom of contemporary societies’ dependence on digital technologies, investments in this infrastructure raise questions about what kind of economic development we are building and which ecological relations take shape in this process. Seeking to understand what kind of value is produced in data centers and how the infrastructure-environment relationship operates within this production, this research proposes understanding data centers as sites of modulation and control of climatic variables oriented toward computation. Above all, in dialogue with data center studies, the political economy of the environment, the anthropology of infrastructure, and entropy studies, this thesis argues that data centers, through the distributed agency between humans and their artifacts, result in a technological ecosystem. Such an ecosystem is a site of experimentation for the production of a type of climate contractually enforced for the operation of servers and other computational devices. It is therefore a mode of capitalist exploitation based on negentropic practices of energetic, thermal, and informational control, such that the resulting commodity is a temporality supposedly “cleansed” of the possibility of catastrophic events. To arrive at these conclusions, the research was based on fieldwork conducted primarily with data center companies and workers located in the metropolitan region of São Paulo. The aim was to understand their worldviews, sociotechnical relations, and the environmental issues that shape the functioning of this infrastructure. Additionally, I participated in trade fairs and events aimed at companies in the sector, which provided me with contact with the discourses and modes of sociability of this milieu. This contact made it possible to identify latent contradictions, as, for example, the tension between the promise of ecologically sustainable development and the need for exponential growth.

---

## 253. [Stablecoin Runs as Safe-Asset Stress Signals: Episode-Level Evidence for Central Bank Surveillance](<https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26180>)

- 作者：Ahmad Qadi; Hui Gong; Francesca Medda
- 发表日期：2026-11-19
- 引用次数：0
- DOI：https://doi.org/10.48550/arxiv.2611.26180
- [打开 PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.26180>)

**摘要：**

This paper asks whether stablecoin runs carry information about U.S. Treasury yields. Using daily data on USDT, USDC and BUSD from 2021 to 2025 and five stress episodes, it measures flows from changes in token units rather than market capitalisation, which removes the phantom inflows the standard measure records when tokens lose their peg. Flows have no stable relationship with yields in normal periods. During the three panic-driven runs, a one-billion-dollar five-day redemption is associated with an 8.5 basis point fall in the three-month bill yield within ten trading days, while two regulatory episodes carry no such signal. The response is broad across maturities and has the opposite sign to what reserve liquidation predicts, which points to flight to quality. The effect is led by the TerraUSD collapse and corroborated by the USDC/SVB run. Five-day redemptions of roughly USD 2 to 3 billion during a panic mark the scale at which stablecoin stress becomes informative for surveillance.

---

## 254. [From Digital Readiness to Economic Development: Blockchain Development, Green Finance and Global Integration in Emerging Asia](<https://doi.org/10.1002/sd.71946>)

- 作者：S.M. Masudur Rahman; Eryadi Kordi Masli; Viet Le
- 发表日期：2026-11-19
- 引用次数：0
- DOI：https://doi.org/10.1002/sd.71946
- [打开 PDF](<https://onlinelibrary.wiley.com/doi/pdfdirect/10.1002/sd.71946>)

**摘要：**

This study examines how blockchain development, green finance and global integration relate to economic development in 10 emerging Asian economies over 2000–2024. Composite indices are constructed from raw indicators using principal component analysis (PCA), with equal weights applied where sampling adequacy is weak. The estimation accounts for cross‐sectional dependence and slope heterogeneity. It combines cross‐sectionally demeaned pooled mean group estimation with common‐factor, fixed‐effects, bias‐corrected dynamic and instrumental‐variable estimators. Green finance shows a robust positive long‐run association with development, driven mainly by domestic sustainable capital formation rather than international climate finance. Blockchain development and global integration support development only in higher‐readiness economies, which reveals a readiness threshold. Global integration does not amplify the effects of digital or green finance. The findings suggest that green finance is development‐compatible across readiness levels, while digital and openness strategies pay off once foundational capacity is in place.

---

## 255. [Antom 3-in-1 Transformer: A Payment Foundation Model](<https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25403>)

- 作者：Weiyu Han; Haoyu Jiang; Jie Sheng; 潘宥西 Pan Youxi; Xianglong Liu; Zeqi Ren; Dongquan Yi; Yinhao Liu; Ruobing Zuo; Ze Zhao; Huilin He; Nairong Zhang; Xinyi He; Xiao Tan; Jun Wang; Pan Fang; Wei Zeng; Jianan Su; Kaixin Shen; Jingtong Wu; Hao Li; Shuai Chen; Jiangming Yang; Tianyi Zhang
- 发表日期：2026-11-19
- 引用次数：0
- DOI：https://doi.org/10.48550/arxiv.2611.25403
- [打开 PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.25403>)

**摘要：**

We report the development of Antom 3-in-1 Transformer, a payment foundation model that integrates sequence, tabular, and graph representations for payment risk detection. The model contains approximately 7B parameters, with approximately 1.7B activated per task route. Antom 3-in-1 Transformer is pretrained to learn reusable behavioral and relational representations, primarily on Ant International's internal payment data. In a cross-business transfer experiment, pretraining improves the target business metric by 34.5% over the same architecture trained from scratch. With only 25% of the training labels, the pretrained model outperforms the full-label scratch baseline in Average Precision (AP) and Recall@0.5% on different merchant domains. In a four-month online experiment on Ant International's billion-scale payment platform, the model reduced the overall loss rate by 37.88% relative to XGB-Online.

---

## 256. [Correctable Enrollment and One-Transaction Seed Proofs for Ethereum's Post-Quantum Migration](<https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25663>)

- 作者：Xin Jin; Maximillian Liao; Handong Chen; Yi Nancy Li; Junyu Liu
- 发表日期：2026-11-19
- 引用次数：0
- DOI：https://doi.org/10.48550/arxiv.2611.25663
- [打开 PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.25663>)

**摘要：**

A quantum computer running Shor's algorithm can extract the private key of every Ethereum account whose public key is published, and accounts exposed by their own transactions hold 54.6% of execution-layer ETH. As the first such machine may stay secret, a migration cannot wait for visible thefts, and once keys are extractable, legacy signatures no longer tell owners from attackers. We design a migration in which owners enroll post-quantum keys cheaply with their existing signatures before ECDSA is retired at a scheduled time, while a zero-knowledge proof of the wallet's BIP-39 seed can correct any such enrollment until a common deadline afterward; an account nobody enrolled stays frozen and recoverable by seed proof without a deadline. Each proof binds the new key, a nonce and a fee cap, so neither a paying sponsor nor a reordering block builder can redirect or replay it. Validators switch to hash-based keys first, at a finalized checkpoint and under a stated bound on the attacker's voting power. We prove the account guarantees under explicit assumptions, model-check the rules, and implement the design in go-ethereum and a Lean consensus client. A three-layer recursive hash-based STARK yields a seed proof of about 190 KB that fits one transaction, verifies in 38 ms and takes a median of 111 s to generate on a laptop; four proofs aggregate into one. In a ten-node simulation with mainnet-derived accounts and fee demand, block space rather than proving sets the duration: the 4.45 million accounts with at least 0.1 ETH would migrate in 29.4 days if all enroll before ECDSA is retired and in 235 days if all recover after it in batches of four. In a mass enrollment, an enrollment waits a median of 19.1 minutes in the public mempool, longer than published estimates for a fast quantum computer to extract its key, so owners with a seed and an unexposed key should migrate by seed proof.

---

## 257. [Path-Independent Deleveraging Rules](<https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26623>)

- 作者：Christoph Schlegel; Akaki Mamageishvili
- 发表日期：2026-11-19
- 引用次数：0
- DOI：https://doi.org/10.48550/arxiv.2611.26623
- [打开 PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.26623>)

**摘要：**

We characterise path-independent auto-deleveraging (ADL) rules as an extended log-exponential family. Immunity to account splitting, merging and reopening further restrict the class. Homogeneity (essentially) restricts the class to a one-parameter family ordered by both fairness and platform risk. Its split-proof members continuously interpolate between proportional division and water-filling. Among these rules, decreasing the parameter reduces risk at the cost of increasing inequality in how accounts are treated. This suggests selecting the parameter through an ex-ante calibration to a risk bound on historical data to choose a maximally fair rule subject to that bound. A counterfactual replay of Hyperliquid's ADL episode on 10 October 2025 finds that a range of intermediate parameters significantly improves on the observed allocation in both burden dispersion and subsequent risk statistics.

---

## 258. [Sleep deprivation remodels hepatic immune microenvironment via circadian gene dysregulation and promotes hepatocellular carcinoma progression](<https://doi.org/10.1080/07420528.2026.2763621>)

- 作者：Boyu Yan; Fangyu Lin; Limin Chen; Peng Ke; Wu Han; Ting Chen
- 发表日期：2026-11-19
- 引用次数：0
- DOI：https://doi.org/10.1080/07420528.2026.2763621

**摘要：**

Sleep deprivation (SD) is a widespread public health issue that may adversely affect liver health. Although SD is a recognized circadian stressor, the mechanisms linking circadian rhythm-related changes to liver injury remain poorly understood. This study aims to systematically investigate the role of circadian rhythm-related genes (CRGs) in mediating SD-induced remodeling of the liver immune microenvironment and its potential implications for hepatocellular carcinoma (HCC). Transcriptomic datasets from the Gene Expression Omnibus (GEO) were analyzed to identify differentially expressed genes (DEGs) following SD. A rat SD model was used to assess hepatic injury via serum alanine aminotransferase (ALT) and aspartate aminotransferase (AST). Functional enrichment analyses and machine learning identified pivotal CRGs. Key genes were validated across datasets and in HCC. Immune infiltration was analyzed using CIBERSORT and the Tumor Immune Estimation Resource (TIMER) database. Experimental validation in a rat SD model demonstrated significant elevations in serum ALT and AST levels, confirming the induction of hepatic injury. SD induced significant alterations in CRGs. CRGs were found to centrally regulate inflammatory, immune, and oncogenic processes. A machine learning framework identified six core CRGs (Cirbp, Srebf1, Fbxo31, Hsp90aa1, Btc, and Dlgap1), whose dysregulated expression was consistently validated. Furthermore, SD led to a significant reshuffling of the hepatic immune landscape, characterized by changes in five distinct immune cell types, which showed strong correlations with the six key CRGs. Notably, among the six hub CRGs, Cirbp and Hsp90aa1 were significantly associated with immune infiltration levels and prognosis in HCC patients. In addition, Serpine1, which was independently identified by LASSO as a key SD-related gene, showed similar associations.

## 259. [Ninth Circuit, 2021-2025: 21 of 82 officer appeals from qualified-immunity denials were reversed in full (25.6%) - preregistration, code and results (S27)](<https://doi.org/10.5281/zenodo.24383614>)

Preregistration, code and derived results for study S27 of the Scaled Legal Legal Lab (agent handle cicero-lex): a check of Ninth Circuit decisions (2021-2025) of a state or local law-enforcement officer's appeal from a pre-judgment denial of qualified immunity, against Schwartz, How Qualified Immunity Fails, 127 Yale L.J. 2 (2017) Table 9. Among 82 CourtListener-indexed decisions, excluding 2 custody cases, 21 were reversed in whole: 21/82 = 0.256 (Wilson 95% CI 0.174 to 0.360) under the frozen denominator (a), and 21/77 = 0.273 (0.186 to 0.381) under the benchmark-matched denominator (b). Prestated label: INCONCLUSIVE (CI includes 0.30) on both denominators. The result is consistent with Table 9 by the preregistered A2 test (S27 lower bounds do not exceed her upper bounds). Status CONFIRMATORY (Coder B agreement gate passed). Process: tier I0. The author context wrote the frozen plan (PREREG.md sha256 12bce4e7205767535bdbaf74dbfcc8ae9f3d2f8271f1cc96c98792fee78a5822; AMENDMENT-01.md sha256 53cd75755aa86f75ccf5827a3be74084863901310be65e325d7c28d8cbb4d1f6, adopted before the harvest). Coder B, a separate agent context of the same model family, coded a 40-unit packet (REVERSED\_WHOLE vs OTHER raw 1.000, kappa 1.000). A separate-context verifier of the same model family returned PASS WITH FIXES; a separate-context re-verify returned PASS. FINDING status: verified-with-fixes. OpenTimestamps proofs of PREREG.md and AMENDMENT-01.md are included. Top-level .ots files are the upgraded copies (Bitcoin-attested at blocks 976640 and 976646). ## 260. [Blockchain in the UK Parliament: An evidence-based study of parliamentary engagement with blockchain, tokenisation, digital assets and DLT](<https://doi.org/10.31585/bba.parliament.2026.1>)

- 作者：未提供
- 发表日期：2026-11-19
- 引用次数：0
- DOI：https://doi.org/10.31585/bba.parliament.2026.1
- [打开 PDF](<https://doi.org/10.31585/bba.parliament.2026.1>)

**摘要：**

This report examines UK parliamentary engagement with blockchain, tokenisation, digital assets and distributed ledger technologies between 1 October 2024 and 31 October 2026. It brings together context-screened Hansard contributions, written questions and answers, legislative and select committee activity, related parliamentary records and verified public engagements. The primary spoken dataset contains 531 relevant contributions by 203 parliamentarians: 111 MPs and 92 members of the House of Lords. Political finance and foreign interference account for 214 spoken records, or 40.3%. The report documents its inclusion rules, attribution, topic coding, coverage limitations and inspectable evidence files. It describes recorded activity rather than measuring expertise, endorsement or societal impact. Produced by the Centre for Evidence-Based Blockchain at The British Blockchain Association. First edition, 19 November 2026.

## 261. [АНАЛИЗ И РАССЛЕДОВАНИЕ МОШЕННИЧЕСТВА В КОНТЕКСТЕ ЦИФРОВИЗАЦИИ](<https://vestnik.sta-jsk.kz/index.php/jsk/article/view/432>)

- 作者：B.R. Ryskulbekova; Беков К.А.; Кошкинбаева А.С.; B.R. Ryskulbekova; Беков К.А.; Кошкинбаева А.С.; Rysskulbekova B.R.; Bekov K.A.; Koshkinbayeva A.S.
- 发表日期：2026-11-19
- 引用次数：0
- DOI：https://doi.org/10.63546/3005-2246.2026.16.4.031
- [打开 PDF](<https://vestnik.sta-jsk.kz/index.php/jsk/article/download/432/299>)

**摘要：**

The active development of digital technologies caused the emergence of new ways of committing fraud, which significantly affected the practice of criminal law qualification and investigation of crimes of this category. The use of remote communication channels, electronic payment instruments, Internet platforms and other digital services has expanded the opportunities of criminals, while at the same time complicating the process of identifying illegal activities and gathering evidence. The purpose of the research is a comprehensive analysis of the features of the criminal-legal qualification of fraud committed using digital technologies, as well as in the determination of actual problems that arise during the investigation of such crimes. Formal-legal, comparative-legal and systematic methods of research were applied in the work, analysis of the current legislation of the Republic of Kazakhstan, scientific publications, materials of law enforcement practice and statistical data was carried out. The results of the research show that effective counteraction to digital fraud requires the improvement of criminal legislation, the introduction of modern methods of digital forensics, the development of interdepartmental and international cooperation, as well as increasing the level of digital literacy of the population. A conclusion is made about the need for a complex approach to prevention, detection and investigation of crimes committed in the digital environment.

## 262. [FLIGHT TO GOLD SEBAGAI RESPONS TERHADAP INFLASI DAN KETIDAKSTABILAN MONETER: ANALISIS PERSPEKTIF AL-MAQRIZI](<https://journal.uin-alauddin.ac.id/index.php/iqtishaduna/article/view/73585>)

- 作者：Riki Maulana; Yadi Janwari; Iwan Setiawan
- 发表日期：2026-11-19
- 引用次数：0
- DOI：https://doi.org/10.24252/iqtishaduna.v8i1.73585
- [打开 PDF](<https://journal.uin-alauddin.ac.id/index.php/iqtishaduna/article/download/73585/27313>)

**摘要：**

Abstrak Penelitian ini bertujuan menganalisis fenomena flight to gold sebagai respons terhadap inflasi dan ketidakstabilan moneter dalam perspektif pemikiran Al-Maqrizi. Fenomena meningkatnya perpindahan aset ke emas terjadi seiring melemahnya nilai mata uang dan meningkatnya jumlah uang beredar yang memicu inflasi. Penelitian ini menggunakan metode kualitatif dengan pendekatan kepustakaan serta analisis konseptual-historis terhadap teori uang dan inflasi Al-Maqrizi. Hasil penelitian menunjukkan bahwa kecenderungan masyarakat beralih ke emas merupakan bentuk rasional dalam menjaga nilai kekayaan akibat menurunnya kepercayaan terhadap uang yang tidak memiliki nilai intrinsik. Hal ini sejalan dengan pandangan Al-Maqrizi bahwa inflasi disebabkan oleh kerusakan sistem moneter, terutama akibat peredaran uang yang berlebihan dan penurunan kualitas mata uang. Emas berfungsi sebagai instrumen penyimpan nilai yang stabil karena memiliki nilai intrinsik. Penelitian ini menyimpulkan bahwa pemikiran Al-Maqrizi relevan dalam menjelaskan fenomena moneter kontemporer serta pentingnya stabilitas moneter untuk menjaga kepercayaan ekonomi. Kata Kunci: Flight to Gold; Inflasi; Ketidakstabilan Moneter; Al-Maqrizi; Standar Emas Abstract This study aims to analyze the "flight to gold" phenomenon as a response to inflation and monetary instability, viewed through the perspective of Al-Maqrizi’s thought. The trend of shifting assets into gold occurs alongside currency depreciation and an expanding money supply that fuels inflation. The study employs a qualitative method, utilizing a literature-based approach and a conceptual-historical analysis of Al-Maqrizi’s theories on money and inflation. The findings indicate that the public's tendency to turn to gold is a rational strategy for preserving wealth amidst declining confidence in money that lacks intrinsic value. This aligns with Al-Maqrizi’s view that inflation stems from a flawed monetary system—specifically one characterized by excessive money circulation and a deterioration in currency quality. Gold serves as a stable store of value due to its intrinsic worth. The study concludes that Al-Maqrizi’s ideas remain relevant for explaining contemporary monetary phenomena and underscore the importance of monetary stability in maintaining economic confidence. Keywords: Flight to Gold; Inflation; Monetary Instability; Al-Maqrizi; Gold Standard

---

## 263. [The impact of monetary policy on the returns of selected crypto-assests in the period 2014-2025](<https://dk.um.si/IzpisGradiva.php?id=100377>)

- 作者：Sebastijan Farkaš
- 发表日期：2026-11-19
- 引用次数：0
- [打开 PDF](<https://dk.um.si/Dokument.php?id=213825&dn=>)

**摘要：**

Kriptosredstva so se v obdobju 2014–2025 razvila iz obrobnega tehnološkega eksperimenta v naložbeni razred, prisoten v portfeljih institucionalnih vlagateljev, bilancah javnih podjetij in na varčevalnih računih milijonov posameznikov po vsem svetu. Ta rast je potekala vzporedno z dvema izrazito različnima fazama monetarne politike: ohlapno politiko med letoma 2009 in 2021 ter agresivnim zaostrovanjem po letu 2021, ko je ameriška centralna banka (Fed) obrestno mero enajstkrat zaporedoma dvignila, ECB pa je sledila s podobno odločnim obratom. Bitcoin je v tem drugem obdobju izgubil več kot 76 odstotkov vrednosti od vrha (“ATH – all time high”). To je zaostrilo že dolgo prisotno vprašanje: ali kriptosredstva, zlasti Bitcoin, dejansko predstavljajo neodvisno zaščito pred razvrednotenjem fiat valut, kot to predpostavlja narativ o "digitalnem zlatu", ali pa so se strukturno vključila v globalni finančni cikel, ki ga oblikuje monetarna politika največjih centralnih bank. Ta magistrska naloga to vprašanje naslavlja s teoretično, pregledno in empirično obravnavo treh kriptosredstev (Bitcoina, etherja in XRP) v razmerju do monetarne politike ameriške centralne banke (Fed) in Evropske centralne banke (ECB). Teoretični del pojasnjuje, da kriptosredstva, ki ne ustvarjajo denarnega toka, po teoriji sedanje vrednosti sodijo med sredstva, ki so najbolj občutljiva na spremembe diskontne stopnje, monetarna politika pa nanje prek kanala cen sredstev, kanala prevzemanja tveganja in iskanja donosa deluje enako kot na druga tvegana finančna sredstva. Pregled literature pokaže, da je zgodnja narativa o Bitcoinu kot varnem zavetju pred inflacijo empirično vse slabše podprta, korelacija z indeksom NASDAQ pa je z vstopom institucionalnih vlagateljev na trg po letu 2020 opazno narasla. Empirični del temelji na dnevnih donosih v obdobju 2014–2025, pri čemer podatkovni vzorec za posamezno kriptosredstvo upošteva njegovo dejansko zgodovino. S korelacijsko analizo po podobdobjih monetarnega cikla in z analizo dogodkov ob odločitvah FOMC in Sveta ECB je preverjena odzivnost donosnosti kriptosredstev na monetarno politiko. Rezultati pokažejo, da se je korelacija kriptosredstev z indeksom NASDAQ 100 postopoma povečevala. V zgodnjem obdobju 2014–2015 je bila pri vseh treh kriptosredstvih statistično neznačilna. V obdobju 2017–2019 je pri etru in XRP dosegla šibko, a statistično značilno raven (0,08–0,09), pri Bitcoinu pa je ostala neznačilna. V restriktivnem obdobju 2022–2023 je nato pri vseh treh narasla na statistično značilno raven 0,35–0,49, kar je najvišja vrednost v celotnem vzorcu. Ta rast ni bila enakomerna: korelacija se je ob propadu borze FTX konec leta 2022 začasno znižala, kar kaže na sposobnost kriptosredstev, da se v kriptospecifičnih šokih vsaj začasno odklopijo od širših tržnih gibanj. Odziv donosnosti na presenečenja monetarne politike je šibkejši, a dosledno v pričakovani, negativni smeri pri vseh testiranih kombinacijah centralna banka–kriptosredstvo, statistično značilen pa predvsem za Bitcoin ob odločitvah Feda. Med kriptosredstvi ostajajo razlike: XRP je skozi celoten vzorec najšibkeje povezan z delniškimi trgi in nepričakovanimi spremembami monetarne politike, ether pa je edino izmed treh, ki prek zastavljanja prinaša reden donos, kar odpira vprašanje o njegovi drugačni dolgoročni občutljivosti na obrestne mere. Ugotovitve skupno kažejo, da so kriptosredstva v veliki meri izgubila zgodnjo neodvisnost od tradicionalnih finančnih trgov, kar ima posledice tako za vlagatelje, ki jih uporabljajo za razpršitev portfelja, kot za regulatorje, ki presojajo njihov pomen za finančno stabilnost.

## 264. [Розширення лексикографічного опису за межі літературного стандарту української мови: ітеративний корпусобазований підхід](<https://lcmp.ukma.edu.ua/article/view/380423>)

- 作者：Vasyl Starko; Андрій Рисін; Марія Шведова
- 发表日期：2026-11-19
- 引用次数：0
- [打开 PDF](<https://lcmp.ukma.edu.ua/article/download/380423/362105>)

**摘要：**

Background. The Ukrainian language has a complicated history of standardization, and different types of variants are widespread in spoken and written Ukrainian today. The existing academic dictionaries are focused on the standard language and do not fully account for all the variation attested in Modern Ukrainian, pointing to an urgent need for better identification and lexicographic description. Contribution to the research field. These findings have important implications for a systemic corpus-based lexicographical description of Modern Ukrainian, particularly the identification and study of the main types of nonstandard and variant lexical items. For the first time, their prevalence in Modern Ukrainian has been ascertained, as represented by their relative frequencies in a large corpus. Tens of thousands of such items have been identified, added to the VESUM dictionary, and tagged in the GRAC corpus, making them accessible for multidimensional scholarly inquiry. Furthermore, the developed iterative, corpus-based approach possesses wide-ranging applicability, providing a pipeline for analyzing various lexical strata in any natural language. Purpose. This study aims to present the results of a multi-year corpus-based project to identify nonstandard items prevalent in Modern Ukrainian, ascertain their frequency characteristics, and provide their lexicographic description in a morphological dictionary. Methods. Semiautomatic corpus analysis has been used to identify candidate items in corpus data, study them in context, and categorize them. Morphological modeling has been applied to assign inflection codes to the new lemmas for the VESUM dictionary. Results. The implemented workflow relies on an iterative co-development of two interlinked, symbiotic resources: a text corpus (GRAC) and a morphological dictionary (VESUM), which are extended and refined during each cycle, incrementally enhancing text coverage and annotation accuracy. As a result, a substantial volume of nonstandard and variant items has been identified in texts and received formal lexicographic description: over 10,000 stylistically deprecated lemmas (3,146 ipm in the GRAC corpus), 7,400 obsolete and dialectal lemmas (936 ipm), nonstandard spelling variants (over 3,300 lemmas and word forms, 2,325 ipm), more than 3,100 slangisms and 750 vulgar or obscene words (397 ipm), variant grammatical forms (1,064 ipm), substandard word forms in over 50,000 paradigms (149 ipm), and rare word forms (over 23,000 paradigms, 88 ipm). Discussion. Each of the above types has been assigned a dedicated tag, and all extracted items are now integrated into VESUM. Consequently, these units are fully searchable within GRAC, accessible for empirical inspection via the dictionary’s web interface, and available for automated annotation by the NLP-UK toolkit. The restrictive tags are used to mark nonstandard items in Pravopysnyk LanguageTool, an advanced Ukrainian text checker that relies on VESUM. By design, VESUM is a descriptive dictionary aimed at maximizing lexical coverage of Ukrainian texts with a layer of prescriptive annotation tailored to Pravopysnyk. Nonstandard and variant words and forms are characteristic of Modern Ukrainian and must continue receiving empirically grounded lexicographical coverage.

---

## 265. [TOUMAII: A 4.5M-Pair Tool-Calling Preference Dataset and Its Source-Agnostic Generator](<https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26722>)

- 作者：Moritz Pötzsch; Andrea Gentilini; Lisa Rossgoderer; Tom Osterfeld; Kesava Prasad Sanjivi Arul; Swanand Kadhe
- 发表日期：2026-11-19
- 引用次数：0
- DOI：https://doi.org/10.48550/arxiv.2611.26722
- [打开 PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.26722>)

**摘要：**

To autonomously execute multi-step tasks, enterprise agents must reliably interact with tools, call APIs, or query databases, making tool-calling a fundamental capability of AI models. Supervised fine-tuning (SFT) optimises for producing the correct call, but never contrasts it with a plausible incorrect one. In an environment where even a minor formatting error can invalidate a call and an incorrect decision can derail the interaction, direct preference optimisation (DPO) targets exactly this contrast. DPO, therefore, requires preference pairs rather than plain demonstrations. For tool-calling, such data, especially in realistic multi-turn settings, remains extremely scarce. To address this scarcity, we introduce TOUMAII, a large-scale tool-calling preference dataset containing ~4.5M validated preference pairs, derived from Agent-Ark/Toucan-1.5M (apache-2.0) conversations. The dataset consists of multi- and single-turn interactions and includes parallel and sequential function calls. Additionally, we release TOUMAII-Gen, a synthetic data generation pipeline that consumes tool-calling SFT datasets and generates a preference dataset with naturally occurring model-generated tool-calling failure modes. To validate the effectiveness of our DPO data, we fine-tune a 3-billion-parameter model, achieving performance parity with other high-quality tool-calling preference datasets. Furthermore, we release a second small preference dataset derived from ToolACE (apache-2.0), showcasing the source-agnostic nature of our pipeline. We release the complete code and datasets: https://github.com/tum-ai/toumaii-gen and https://huggingface.co/collections/tum-ai/toumaii-generated-datasets

---

## 266. [Cross-Border Cryptoasset Flows and Stablecoin Payments: Opportunities, Challenges and Implications for Developing Countries](<https://doi.org/10.5772/intechopen.1018652>)

- 作者：François d’Assises Babou Bationo; Ruffin Adja
- 发表日期：2026-11-19
- 引用次数：0
- DOI：https://doi.org/10.5772/intechopen.1018652
- [打开 PDF](<https://www.intechopen.com/citation-pdf-url/1253068>)

**摘要：**

Among the technologies emerging in the twenty-first century are distributed ledger technologies, on which digital assets are based. Recent developments indicate significant growth in cryptoasset markets and a growing connection with traditional finance, which pave the way for facilitating cross-border payments and thus enhancing financial integration between countries. We examine cross-border cryptoasset flows, with particular emphasis on the role of stablecoins in cross-border payments. We first provide a literature review discussing opportunities and challenges related to cross-border cryptoasset flows, mainly the monetary economics of cryptoassets and their impacts on cross-border payments. Second, we provide an original empirical analysis of cross-border cryptoasset transactions considering all tokens exchanged on the Ethereum Blockchain. Our empirical findings show a high degree of concentration in Ethereum transactions across participants, geographic flows, and cryptoassets, with a particularly strong concentration in dollar-pegged stablecoins in cross-border payments. Finally, we discuss the implications for developing countries. We argue that distributed ledger technologies represent an innovative structural transformation that could transform financial systems, and that regulated cryptoassets, mainly stablecoins, can boost user confidence and promote their effective use in cross-border payment transactions.

---

## 267. [Compiler.next: A Search-Based Compiler to Power the AI-Native Future of Software Engineering](<https://doi.org/10.1145/3802581>)

- 作者：Filipe R. Cogo; Gustavo A. Oliva; Ahmed E. Hassan
- 发表日期：2026-11-18
- 引用次数：3
- DOI：https://doi.org/10.1145/3802581

**摘要：**

The rapid advancement of AI-assisted software engineering has brought transformative potential to the field of software engineering, but existing tools and paradigms remain limited by cognitive overload, inefficient tool integration, and the narrow capabilities of AI copilots. In response, we propose Compiler.next, a novel search-based compiler designed to enable the seamless evolution of AI-native software systems as part of the emerging Software Engineering 3.0 era. Unlike traditional static compilers, Compiler.next takes human-written intents and automatically generates working software by searching for an optimal solution. This process involves dynamic optimization of cognitive architectures and their constituents (e.g., prompts, foundation model configurations, and system parameters) while finding the optimal tradeoff between several objectives, such as accuracy, cost, and latency. This article outlines the architecture of Compiler.next and positions it as a cornerstone in democratizing software development by lowering the technical barrier for non-experts, enabling scalable, adaptable, and reliable AI-powered software. We present a roadmap to address the core challenges in intent compilation, including developing quality programming constructs, effective search heuristics, reproducibility, and interoperability between compilers. Our vision lays the groundwork for fully automated, search-driven software development, fostering faster innovation and more efficient AI-driven systems.

---

## 268. [Enigma Root of Trust: A Third-Party Notary for National and Corporate Ledgers](<https://doi.org/10.66693/iaic.tr.2026.2>)

- 作者：Andrey Vitalievich Nechesov
- 发表日期：2026-11-18
- 引用次数：2
- DOI：https://doi.org/10.66693/iaic.tr.2026.2
- [打开 PDF](<https://enigma.ist/downloads/reports/iaic/2026/2/IAIC-TR-2026-02.pdf>)

**摘要：**

Many records must be trusted by parties who share no owner: an export certificate issued in one country and checked on arrival in another, a mining licence checked by a buyer, a supplier's declaration checked by a regulator. Enigma Root of Trust is a ledger operated by a neutral third party into which registered members (ministries, agencies and companies) write cryptographic fingerprints of their own records. A member's records never leave the member: it writes a Merkle root of a period's records, signed with its own keys, so the operator can order and preserve commitments but cannot create or alter them. The root is the Enigma Blockchain of IAIC-TR-2026-01, with no coin, no token and no fees, anchored in the Bitcoin blockchain through OpenTimestamps. The report specifies the roles of operator, member, witness and verifier, the commitment format and acceptance rules, daily anchoring and a fallback against censorship by the operator, the verification algorithm, corridors between members under which a certificate can be received only once, contract interfaces, rules enforced by smart contracts and checked for all inputs, AI-assisted monitoring that flags but never decides, the threat model and the implementation status. It applies the design to a verified gold corridor between Ghana and the United Arab Emirates, built on top of whichever traceability system Ghana selects, and closes with a six-week proof-of-concept plan.

---

## 269. [Authenticating IoT Data on Ethereum after Pectra and Fusaka: A Cost Study of Signature Schemes, Batching and Anchoring for Agricultural Supply Chain Finance](<https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23764>)

- 作者：Ming-Fong Sie; Jian-Hong Chen; Gwan-Yen Lin; Yen-Shuo Chen; Shih-Wei Liao
- 发表日期：2026-11-18
- 引用次数：1
- DOI：https://doi.org/10.48550/arxiv.2611.23764
- [打开 PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.23764>)

**摘要：**

Blockchain-based supply chain finance can turn the sensor-recorded production history of smallholder farms into verifiable credit evidence, but only if every record is authenticated and anchored at a cost that scales to large device fleets. Ethereum's 2025 Pectra and Fusaka upgrades changed this cost landscape: they added precompiles for BLS12-381 and P-256, repriced calldata and MODEXP, and capped transaction gas at $2^{24}$. We organize the authentication and anchoring of IoT records into three trust levels, from posting a Merkle root through a trusted aggregator to verifying every device signature on-chain, and implement four replay-protected signature designs: ECDSA over secp256k1 and P-256, BLS aggregate signatures with on-chain hash-to-curve, and a pseudonymous identity-based signature with batch verification. Measured as signed transactions under the rules before and after both upgrades, the upgrades mainly changed which designs are feasible, not their cost. Batching and Merkle anchoring cut the cost of ECDSA authentication sixfold, to 8,963 gas per record, and secp256k1 ECDSA remains the cheapest authenticated design: P-256 costs 1.6 times as much, the identity-based design 2.7 times and BLS aggregation 3.9 times, dominated by on-chain hashing to the curve. One transaction carries at most 1,754 ECDSA-authenticated records but only 411 BLS-aggregated ones. On a Raspberry Pi Zero 2 W, pairing-based signing takes 5.7 ms against 0.29 ms for ECDSA. Pairing-based designs pay off only when the application needs their additional properties, and the identity-based design adds key escrow at the key generation centre. The benchmark harness is released for reproducibility.

---

## 270. [From Punchlines to Politics: The Joe Rogan Experience as a Case Study of the Politicization of Apolitical Spaces in the U.S](<https://doi.org/10.1080/15205436.2026.2758779>)

- 作者：Huma Rasheed; Liam Cuddy; Brooke Molokach; Jiwon Nam; Scarlett Feuerstein; Lance Holbert; Dannagal Goldthwaite Young
- 发表日期：2026-11-18
- 引用次数：0
- DOI：https://doi.org/10.1080/15205436.2026.2758779
- [打开 PDF](<https://www.tandfonline.com/doi/pdf/10.1080/15205436.2026.2758779?needAccess=true>)

**摘要：**

The Joe Rogan Experience (JRE), one of the most widely consumed media products in the world, has evolved from a podcast with comedic roots to a platform of political significance. This transformation of JRE exemplifies a broader trend of politicization of traditionally apolitical spaces in the United States that may have consequential political implications. To examine this shift and its relationship with political outcomes, this paper presents findings from two studies: Study 1 is a content analysis of JRE episodes aired through December 2024 (N = 2,175 transcripts) that demonstrates a rise in socio-political content on the show. Study 2 draws on a survey (N = 1,600) to assess the relationship between JRE exposure and voting behavior in the 2024 presidential election. In light of these findings, we discuss how emerging media logics are shaping contemporary political discourse.

## 271. [SatForensics: a transformer-based multi-modal framework for cyber-attack detection, attribution, and chain-of-custody forensics in low-earth-orbit satellite communication networks](<https://doi.org/10.1038/s41598-026-81042-7>)

- 作者：Keshav Kaushik; Mahran M. Al-Zyoud; Priyanka Gaur; Gaurav Rajput; Manpreet Singh; Rahul Joshi
- 发表日期：2026-11-18
- 引用次数：0
- DOI：https://doi.org/10.1038/s41598-026-81042-7
- [打开 PDF](<https://www.nature.com/articles/s41598-026-81042-7_reference.pdf>)

**摘要：**

Low-Earth-Orbit (LEO) satellite networks lack public records of labelled cyber-attacks, which makes it hard to evaluate detection, attribution and forensic tools for them. We present SatForensics, a gated multi-modal Transformer that scores each satellite observation for anomalies, attributes injected RF attacks to one of five archetypes (broadband, continuous-wave, swept and pulsed jamming, and Doppler-following spoofing), and records every event in a SHA-256 hash-chained ledger together with a calibrated posterior, a confidence margin and an ambiguity flag. The model fuses RF features of the recorded waterfall, orbital geometry propagated from the observation’s own orbital elements, and link context. We evaluate it on 5,408 real SatNOGS observations from 573 satellites and 379 stations, using satellite-grouped splits, attacks injected after splitting, and 5 runs reported as mean ± standard deviation. SatForensics reaches a detection F1 of 81.0 \\(\\pm \\) 1.4% and an attribution macro-F1 of 96.1 \\(\\pm \\) 0.7%. It outperforms SVM, CNN and LSTM baselines in detection but not Random Forest (83.5 \\(\\pm \\) 1.4%) or an RF-only Transformer (84.3 \\(\\pm \\) 1.6%), and ablations show no reliable contribution of gating, geometry, context or mutual-information alignment on this data. Natural labels are SatNOGS vetting outcomes, not attacks, and attack labels are synthetic. Ledger appends take a median of 10.0 \\(\\mu \\) s and inference takes 0.47 ms on one CPU thread. The code and dataset construction scripts are released.

## 273. [A lag-aware and interpretable two-stage framework for identifying dominant precipitation-related predictors in the data-scarce Mekong River Basin](<https://doi.org/10.1016/j.ejrh.2026.104207>)

- 作者：Xi Weng; Tianshan Zeng; Yiqun Sun; Dongdong Gao; Qiongfang Li; Xiaogang Tian; Nengmin Zhu; Hongle Shi
- 发表日期：2026-11-18
- 引用次数：0
- DOI：https://doi.org/10.1016/j.ejrh.2026.104207
- [打开 PDF](<https://www.sciencedirect.com/science/article/pii/S2214581826011079/pdf>)

**摘要：**

Study area The Mekong River Basin is a data-scarce transboundary basin where sparse, unevenly distributed, and temporally discontinuous gauge observations hinder reliable precipitation estimation, particularly under strong subregional hydroclimatic heterogeneity. Research focus This study developed a lag-aware and interpretable two-stage framework to identify dominant precipitation-related predictors across the basin. In the first stage, transfer entropy (TE) was used to preliminarily screen candidate predictors by assessing directional lagged dependence from each candidate variable to precipitation over 1–7-day lags. In the second stage, tree-based machine learning and SHapley Additive exPlanations (SHAP) were used to quantify predictor contributions within the fitted models and diagnose predictor structures across subregions and rainfall regimes. New hydrological insights The framework reduced a high-dimensional candidate predictor space to compact sets of 10–15 inputs. Under a common sample-based split, the compact models achieved a mean Nash–Sutcliffe efficiency of 0.232, compared with 0.275 for models using the complete preliminarily screened sets, and performed better overall than conventional feature-selection alternatives. Across subregions, cloud microphysical variables aloft and lower-atmospheric moisture variables were recurring contributors to modelled precipitation. Convective available potential energy (CAPE) and directional transport-related predictors also played important roles. In the heavy-precipitation subset (≥90 mm day−1), transport-, flux-, and energy-related predictors became more prominent in the fitted-model SHAP rankings. This rainfall-regime contrast provides a useful diagnostic of regional precipitation processes, although the heavy-precipitation results remain exploratory because of the limited number of gauge-day samples.

## 274. [Code and results for: Explainable Graph Learning Automata: A Unified Framework for Adaptive Decision Intelligence in Dynamic Networks](<https://doi.org/10.5281/zenodo.24334083>)

- 作者：R. Krishna Kumari
- 发表日期：2026-11-18
- 引用次数：0
- DOI：https://doi.org/10.5281/zenodo.24334083

**摘要：**

This record contains the source code, configuration and complete experimental results supporting the manuscript “Explainable Graph Learning Automata: A Unified Framework for Adaptive Decision Intelligence in Dynamic Networks”, prepared for Springer Lecture Notes in Networks and Systems. EGLA (Explainable Graph Learning Automata) predicts bounded trust ratings in a temporal signed network. Each user carries a set of learning automata: a bank of trust-level automata mixed by a learning-rate automaton, plus rater and reliability automata. All of them are updated by reward and penalty as ratings arrive. Their states are combined with signed temporal neighbourhood evidence in a bounded read-out whose output decomposes exactly into feature groups. An exact replay engine recomputes any decision with evidence removed, which gives counterfactual explanations. The experiments use the Stanford SNAP Bitcoin OTC trust network (5,881 users; 35,592 ratings from −10 to +10; 2010–2016) under a chronological 70/10/20 test-then-train protocol. The record includes: the data-audit and preprocessing script and the EGLA implementation; the baselines: mean predictors, neighbourhood aggregation, online Fairness–Goodness, a TGN in PyTorch Geometric, and a gradient-boosted read-out; hyper-parameter search, ablations, explainability evaluation (fidelity, stability, consistency) and runtime measurements; all predictions and logs, and the scripts that generate every figure and table. The Bitcoin OTC data are not redistributed here. The code is released under the MIT License, and the results, figures and documentation under CC BY 4.0. Please cite the paper and the dataset papers listed on the SNAP Bitcoin OTC page.

## 277. [GCR-0001 — Génération de clés RSA à graine rejouable. Avant-projet de norme, version 0.1](<https://doi.org/10.5281/zenodo.24356090>)

- 作者：Honoré DEMBELE
- 发表日期：2026-11-18
- 引用次数：0
- DOI：https://doi.org/10.5281/zenodo.24356090

**摘要：**

Résumé. La présente norme fixe la manière de fabriquer une clé RSA. Elle ne change rien à RSA : une clé conforme est une clé RSA ordinaire, acceptée par tout logiciel existant. Elle fixe une seule chose, la génération : les deux nombres premiers sont construits à partir d'une graine, par un calcul entièrement décrit, de sorte que la même graine redonne la même clé sur n'importe quelle machine et avec n'importe quel programme écrit d'après le texte. Celui qui détient une clé conforme peut donc faire la preuve qu'elle sort du calcul public et de rien d'autre. Le but est que des autorités de certification africaines délivrent des certificats X.509 ordinaires dont les clés ont été fabriquées par un générateur maîtrisé de bout en bout. La construction des premiers est celle des premiers prouvables de Shawe-Taylor (FIPS 186-4, annexes B.3.2, C.6 et C.10) ; la norme la rend obligatoire, la décrit sans renvoi, y joint une fiche de génération et définit sept contrôles de conformité. Abstract. This standard fixes how an RSA key is made. It changes nothing in RSA: a conforming key is an ordinary RSA key, accepted by all existing software. It fixes one thing only, generation: the two primes are built from a seed, by a computation described in full, so that the same seed gives the same key on any machine and with any program written from the text. Whoever holds a conforming key can therefore prove that it comes from the public computation and from nothing else. The aim is for African certification authorities to issue ordinary X.509 certificates whose keys were made by a generator mastered from end to end. The construction of the primes is the Shawe-Taylor provable prime construction (FIPS 186-4, appendices B.3.2, C.6 and C.10); the standard makes it mandatory, describes it without cross-reference, attaches a generation record to it and defines seven conformity checks. Status. Preliminary draft (avant-projet de norme), version 0.1 of 18 November 2026, open for public comment. The computation has been checked against the official NIST ACVP vectors for this method, and two implementations written separately give the same keys. Files. The French version is the reference; the English file is its translation, and in case of discrepancy the French version prevails. Both files carry the author's handwritten signature and an electronic signature (PAdES). Each file, and each signature certificate, is anchored on the Bitcoin blockchain through OpenTimestamps (blocks 976480 to 976487, 18 November 2026).

## 278. [A Rate-Cumulative Valuation Model for Bitcoin, with a Next-Cycle Forecast and an Out-of-Sample Backtest](<https://doi.org/10.5281/zenodo.24349247>)

**摘要：**

Draft preprint, not peer reviewed. We model the Bitcoin price as the ratio of two on-chain flows, monthly dollar volume and monthly bitcoin volume, each expressed as a power law of cumulative dollar volume, in the manner of the petroleum engineer's rate-cumulative (decline-curve) analysis. Includes a structural break search (June 2015), a damped cycle factor with a next-cycle forecast, and out-of-sample and rolling-origin backtests.

## 279. [Empirical Study on Stock Market Fluctuation Prediction Using NLP-Based Sentiment Analysis](<https://doi.org/10.37394/23209.2027.24.7>)

- 作者：Nguyen Minh Tuan; Lam Duy Quy; Nguyen Huynh Minh Luan; Phayung Meesad; Nguyen Hong Son
- 发表日期：2026-11-18
- 引用次数：0
- DOI：https://doi.org/10.37394/23209.2027.24.7
- [打开 PDF](<https://wseas.com/journals/isa/2027/a145109-1273.pdf>)

**摘要：**

This paper investigates the relationship between stock market fluctuations and natural language sentiment extracted from social media. Twitter data are analyzed to derive sentiment signals associated with major stocks, which are then aligned with historical price data collected from Yahoo Finance. The study focuses on three main tasks: (i) synchronizing sentiment information with stock price movements, (ii) analyzing the impact of sentiment fluctuations on individual stock volatility, and (iii) forecasting the Vietnamese stock market index (VN-Index) based on sentiment-driven price predictions. Machine learning and deep learning models, including Long Short-Term Memory (LSTM) networks and hybrid architectures such as CNN-BiLSTM with attention mechanisms, are employed to improve temporal variables between sentiment signals and market dynamics. Experimental results on data from 2021 to 2025 demonstrate a noticeable correlation between sentiment scores and stock price movements for companies such as Apple (AAPL) and Amazon (AMZN). The proposed models show competitive performance in predicting future stock trends and VN-Index movements in 2026 and 2027. The obtained results demonstrate that integrating sentiment analysis with time-series modeling provides valuable insights into market volatility and improves predictive accuracy. This work illustrates the potential of natural language-driven approaches as effective tools for stock market analysis and decision support.

---

## 280. [Urd: A Pipelined Blockchain Sharding Protocol with Cooperative Cross-Shard Verification](<https://doi.org/10.1007/s41019-026-00369-3>)

- 作者：Hao Dong; Hengtai Zhao; Ziyao Wang; Lianpeng Qiao; Ye Yuan; Aoqian Zhang
- 发表日期：2026-11-18
- 引用次数：0
- DOI：https://doi.org/10.1007/s41019-026-00369-3
- [打开 PDF](<https://link.springer.com/content/pdf/10.1007/s41019-026-00369-3.pdf>)

**摘要：**

Sharding is a promising approach to enhancing blockchain scalability, and it has recently attracted widespread attention. In order to ensure the security of sharding blockchains, many existing systems that utilize non-cooperative consensus randomly allocate a large number of nodes to each shard, which poses difficulties for deployment in real-world scenarios. Most of the current cooperative consensus protocols depend on a global consensus. This reliance introduces additional consensus steps and restricts their scalability. Furthermore, the communication overhead in these protocols is significant and their transaction processing parallelism is low. In this paper, we propose CoCSV, a decentralized cross-shard verification protocol consisting of three phases.Specifically, it features a VERIFY phase, during which shard leaders exchange their Commitment Intentions regarding blocks generated by other shards. To enhance the protocol performance, we then introduce Urd, a 6P-3PC protocol achieving a sixfold increase in parallelism. It allows a cross-shard transaction to commit in just one voting round on average. We provide a theoretical analysis to demonstrate that our protocol can achieve a high level of security when compared to existing non-cooperative protocols. Finally, we implement a prototype of Urd using Go and conduct experiments on up to 440 nodes. Our evaluation demonstrates that the proposed protocol increases the transaction throughput by 2.877 times when there are 11 shards and 440 nodes, while incurring only 9.6% more bandwidth overhead for cross-shard verification.

---

## 281. [Sharia compliance and trading psychology: A phenomenological study of Indonesian Muslim cryptocurrency investors](<https://doi.org/10.1016/j.ssaho.2026.104070>)

- 作者：Muhammad Rizky Amin; Yunieta Anny Nainggolan; Raden Aswin Rahadi
- 发表日期：2026-11-18
- 引用次数：0
- DOI：https://doi.org/10.1016/j.ssaho.2026.104070
- [打开 PDF](<https://www.sciencedirect.com/science/article/pii/S2590291126016384/pdf>)

**摘要：**

This study examines how Islamic finance principles moderate cryptocurrency trading psychology among Indonesian Muslim retail investors, addressing a critical gap in behavioral finance research at the intersection of religious ethics and digital investment behavior. Employing Interpretative Phenomenological Analysis (IPA), semi-structured interviews were conducted with eighteen strategically selected participants including retail traders, platform operators, Islamic scholars, regulators, and financial analysts. Five overarching themes emerged: (1) investment fundamentals and religious legitimacy, distinguishing Bitcoin as halal investment from altcoin speculation as potential gambling; (2) platform design and psychological manipulation through behavioral bias exploitation; (3) Islamic finance perspectives and fatwa contestation, revealing fatwa shopping where investors selectively adopt interpretations aligned with profit motivations; (4) risk management strategies demonstrating religious principles as self-regulatory mechanisms; and (5) regulatory and institutional legitimacy mapping evolving moral-legal boundaries. Contrary to theoretical expectations that religiosity restrains speculative behavior, findings reveal religiosity functions as a flexible interpretive framework that legitimizes market participation, transforming moral constraints into cognitive justification for profit-seeking. This study provides the first phenomenological examination of how Indonesian Muslim investors navigate contested religious-ethical terrain in cryptocurrency trading, contributing to behavioral finance literature by revealing how religious accountability frameworks moderate psychological biases, and offering insights for regulators, Islamic scholars, and fintech platforms in Muslim-majority emerging markets.

## 283. [Statistically Detectable, Economically Small: A Pre-Registered Out-of-Sample Test of Minute-Level Bitcoin Return Predictability](<https://doi.org/10.5281/zenodo.24330978>)

- 作者：Gaoshan Zeng
- 发表日期：2026-11-18
- 引用次数：0
- DOI：https://doi.org/10.5281/zenodo.24330978

**摘要：**

We test whether a fixed forecasting program built from public one-minute candles of Binance BTCUSDT spot carries out-of-sample information about 15-minute returns, and how much that information is worth. The program (fourteen causal features, Ridge regression, expanding window) and its tests were frozen and hashed before the evaluation data were read. A historical external validation over 56 monthly folds (2021-2025, 2.45 million minutes) rejects the null of no predictive information (t=5.44), but the evidence is uneven: 76% of the summed Clark-West differential comes from 2021 (excluding 2021, t=3.00), and a test on the 56 monthly means gives t=2.14. A within-month demeaned test (t=5.74) shows that the forecasts carry timing information rather than agreement with the sample trend. Two confirmatory holdouts, each run under a protocol frozen before its data were opened, split. July-August 2026 rejects (t=1.89, p=0.029). September-October 2026, with a drift-free primary statistic, does not (t=1.33, p=0.092), although its estimate (an information coefficient of 0.021) is close to the historical one (0.024). Economically the information is small: squared-error tests never reject, the 95% upper bound on the out-of-sample R^2 is about 0.15-0.2%, and a non-overlapping 15-minute rule breaks even at 0.2-0.3 basis points per side, against a retail taker fee of 10 basis points. A census of 2,070 days of Binance archives finds 157 minutes where the exchange's monthly and daily files disagree; excluding them leaves the results unchanged. Short-horizon predictability of BTCUSDT from public candles is weak, fragile in short samples and economically small.

---

## 284. [GCR-0002 — Autorité de certification à clés rejouables. Avant-projet de norme, version 0.1](<https://doi.org/10.5281/zenodo.24356094>)

- 作者：Honoré DEMBELE
- 发表日期：2026-11-18
- 引用次数：0
- DOI：https://doi.org/10.5281/zenodo.24356094

**摘要：**

Résumé. La présente norme dit comment une autorité de certification africaine se crée, garde ses clés et délivre des certificats. Les certificats sont des certificats X.509 ordinaires : ils servent aux sites, au courrier, à la signature de documents et de code, à l'identité, et sont lus par tout logiciel existant. Ce qui distingue une telle autorité tient en trois points : toutes ses clés sortent du générateur de GCR-0001 et ont été rejouées avant de servir ; sa racine naît au cours d'une cérémonie écrite d'avance, devant témoins, sur une machine sans réseau ; sa chaîne de certificats suit un profil que douze contrôles, exécutables par un programme, suffisent à vérifier. Abstract. This standard states how an African certification authority is created, keeps its keys and issues certificates. The certificates are ordinary X.509 certificates: they serve websites, mail, document and code signing, and identity, and are read by all existing software. What sets such an authority apart comes down to three points: all its keys come from the generator of GCR-0001 and were replayed before being used; its root is born during a ceremony written in advance, before witnesses, on a machine with no network; its certificate chain follows a profile that twelve checks, runnable by a program, are enough to verify. Status. Preliminary draft (avant-projet de norme), version 0.1 of 18 November 2026, open for public comment. A reference tool builds a conforming chain that passes the twelve checks and that OpenSSL verifies in strict mode; revocation lists and the public register are specified but not yet implemented. Files. The French version is the reference; the English file is its translation, and in case of discrepancy the French version prevails. Both files carry the author's handwritten signature and an electronic signature (PAdES). Each file, and each signature certificate, is anchored on the Bitcoin blockchain through OpenTimestamps (blocks 976480 to 976487, 18 November 2026).

## 285. [Stateless Post-Quantum Threshold Signatures and Knowledge-Asymmetry Frozen UTXO Rescue for Blockchain Custody and Legacy Supply Migration](<https://doi.org/10.5281/zenodo.24341313>)

- 作者：Laudza Kusuma
- 发表日期：2026-11-18
- 引用次数：0
- DOI：https://doi.org/10.5281/zenodo.24341313

**摘要：**

Institutional cryptocurrency custodians and legacy unspent transaction outputs face two unresolved threats during the transition to post-quantum cryptography. First, institutional custody engines operate distributed signing servers across isolated availability zones and restore key material from cold backups, making stateful post-quantum signature schemes unsafe due to counter desynchronization and state reuse vulnerabilities. Second, roughly 34 percent of the Bitcoin supply resides in legacy addresses vulnerable to quantum public-key recovery or scheduled sunsetting under draft BIP-361. This paper introduces two protocol constructions built on the Adaptive Quantum Migration Protocol (AQMP v2.0). First, we present a Stateless Post-Quantum Threshold Multi-Party Computation (PQ-MPC) signature engine over module lattices (q = 8,380,417) using synthetic pseudorandom nonce derivation bound to message digests, share commitments, active quorum identifiers, and backup-safe epoch salts. Empirical benchmarks confirm zero mutable signing state across cold-backup wipe and restore events, constant-time verification between 3.90 ms and 4.68 ms across 2-of-3, 3-of-5, and 7-of-11 quorums, and a 99.71 percent block footprint reduction at N = 250 transactions (11,300 bytes via Plonky3 KeyRegistry aggregation versus 3,945,750 bytes for unaggregated 3-of-5 ML-DSA-65). Second, we propose a Knowledge-Asymmetry Frozen UTXO Rescue protocol that proves possession of unhardened BIP-32 parent chain codes and pre-compromise derivation paths in zero knowledge without exposing raw elliptic-curve public keys on-chain. Bound to a 96-byte Dual-Commit XOR cycle and the consensus confirmation deadline ceil(4 / (1 - epsilon)), the complete rescue payload requires 676 bytes, reducing on-chain witness overhead by 80.23 percent compared to Lifeboat (3,420 bytes), 82.68 percent compared to Dropkick (3,904 bytes), and 87.15 percent compared to naive ML-DSA-65 (5,261 bytes).

## 286. [HCR for statistical verification of folk regularities](<https://doi.org/10.5281/zenodo.24348796>)

- 作者：Jarek Duda
- 发表日期：2026-11-18
- 引用次数：0

**摘要：**

Folk regularities like full Moon, birth month or weather lore are usually dismissed or "confirmed" by small studies - here tested on public data with HCR joint distributions and 8 Bitcoin-timestamped preregistrations: most vanish, some are real with mundane mechanisms (relative age, heat, cold weeks, daylight saving).

## 287. [Logos vs. pathos: Which speaks louder in financial markets?](<https://doi.org/10.1016/j.iref.2026.106026>)

- 作者：Zhuo Chen; Yunmiao Zhang; Honghai Yu; Qichen Zhou
- 发表日期：2026-11-18
- 引用次数：0
- DOI：https://doi.org/10.1016/j.iref.2026.106026
- [打开 PDF](<https://www.sciencedirect.com/science/article/pii/S1059056026011408/pdf>)

**摘要：**

What kinds of posts attract attention, and which ones actually motivate investors to trade? This is important for retail investors. Using five million posts from Snowball, a Chinese stock forum, we separate two dimensions of textual style: emotional punctuation (pathos) and logical structure (logos). We find that emotional pathos attracts more broadcast-oriented engagement and is associated with higher trading volume but carries little information, making it more like attention-driven noise. Logos draws more retention-oriented engagement, contains information about firm fundamentals, and thus has strong predictive power for both short- and long-term abnormal returns. The good performance of the long–short portfolio also shows the strong profitability of the logos strategy. Accordingly, sentiment is informative mainly when it is accompanied by logical structure.

---

## 288. [Kick targeting in fast iterated local search: code, data and preregistered run records](<https://doi.org/10.5281/zenodo.24347381>)

- 作者：Ufuk Asıl
- 发表日期：2026-11-18
- 引用次数：0
- DOI：https://doi.org/10.5281/zenodo.24347381

Reproduction package for the paper 'Vuruşları Hedeflemek İçin Öğrenmek Gerekir mi? - 发表日期：2026-11-18

**摘要：**

Every row points to a JSON file timestamped into Bitcoin with OpenTimestamps, so the time a call existed can be checked without trusting us; the column sealed\_before\_resolution marks whether that timestamp predates the outcome. ## 290. [Synchronised Exuberance or Contagion? Date-Stamping and Validating Speculative Bubbles Across Cryptocurrencies and Energy-Transition Assets](<https://doi.org/10.3390/ijfs14110323>)

- 作者：Bashar Yaser Almansour; Seyed Amirhossein Shojaei; Firas Armosh; Bernard Ofosu Boateng
- 发表日期：2026-11-18
- 引用次数：0
- DOI：https://doi.org/10.3390/ijfs14110323

**摘要：**

This study examines whether speculative bubbles in cryptocurrency markets are transmitted to the assets that finance the energy transition, or whether both segments respond to common drivers. Using freely available weekly and daily data from January 2020 to September 2026, it applies the generalised sup ADF procedure with Monte Carlo and wild-bootstrap critical values, separates global explosiveness, exploratory local crossings and independently validated bubbles, and benchmarks the dating against ex-ante log-periodic power-law singularity (LPPLS) diagnostics and ε-drawdown target events. Synchronisation, causality and dynamic probit tests are calibrated by circular-shift, bootstrap and simulation designs and conditioned on measured common factors. Only Bitcoin’s 2020–2021 run-up survives family-wise control; validated bubbles occur in Bitcoin, Ether and clean-energy equities in 2020–2021 and in battery equities in 2025–2026. Crypto and clean-energy exuberance overlapped in a single joint episode, but the overlap is not significant after multiplicity correction, and no specification shows crypto exuberance predicting green exuberance; the 2017 crypto bubble and the 2024 crypto exuberance had no green counterpart. Detection power is limited and lead–lag orderings are not robust. The evidence rejects crypto-to-green contagion but does not identify a common driver, and is best read as a descriptive account of one shared episode.

---

## 291. [AI Powered Criminal Network Analysis System](<https://doi.org/10.48175/ijarsct-40830>)

- 作者：Rohit Rahul Chayal Abhishek Balasaheb Gaikwad; Pravej Raju Attar Rohit Rajesh Ghallal; H. B. Jadhav, Dr. H. B. Jadhav, Dr. H. B. Jadhav
- 发表日期：2026-11-18
- 引用次数：0
- DOI：https://doi.org/10.48175/ijarsct-40830
- [打开 PDF](<https://doi.org/10.48175/ijarsct-40830>)

**摘要：**

Criminal investigations increasingly involve large volumes of linked data such as call records, financial transactions, case reports and social media activity, yet investigators still analyse these relationships largely by hand. This paper presents the design of an AI powered criminal network analysis system built in Python. The system ingests structured and unstructured records, extracts people, organisations, phones, locations and accounts using natural language processing (spaCy), builds a relationship graph (NetworkX), measures the importance of each actor with centrality metrics, detects groups with community detection, suggests hidden or missing links with machine learning, and presents the result as an interactive graph with an investigator-readable report. The system is organised as a linear five-stage pipeline that is modular and can be run on modest hardware. The paper describes the architecture, technology stack, processing flow, applications, advantages and limitations, and discusses ethical and privacy safeguards, a planned evaluation methodology and future work including graph neural networks, multilingual (Hindi and Marathi) text processing and temporal network analysis. No measured performance results are claimed in this paper

## 292. [Conzetian Anomalous Systems and Frameworks: Mathematical Proofs, Tensor Routing Mechanics, and Cryptographic State Anchoring for the 420^7 Sovereign Lattice](<https://doi.org/10.5281/zenodo.24351477>)

- 作者：Justin Neal Thomas Conzet
- 发表日期：2026-11-18
- 引用次数：0
- DOI：https://doi.org/10.5281/zenodo.24351477

**摘要：**

This white paper establishes the rigorous mathematical foundations of the Conzetian Anomalous Architecture, a zero-friction (f = 0.0) execution framework deployed on mobile edge substrates (Samsung Galaxy A17, Termux, SQLite WAL). By formalizing the Starcut Constant through tetrahedral harmonic intersections (19.47 degrees) and golden ratio scaling (Phi), we prove mathematically that spatial coordinate routing achieves constant-time O(1) state resolution across a 420^7 coordinate lattice. Furthermore, we provide complete verification equations for immutable state anchoring across Bitcoin OpenTimestamps (OTS) and Arweave AO permaweb networks. ## 293. [Algorithmic Market Making Can Lead to Rough Volatility Through the Order Book](<https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24028>)

- 作者：Sai Niranjan Ramachandran
- 发表日期：2026-11-18
- 引用次数：0
- DOI：https://doi.org/10.48550/arxiv.2611.24028
- [打开 PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.24028>)

**摘要：**

Existing work suggests that markets can show volatility with paths rougher than those of Brownian motion. In theory this can happen when the order flow is close to instability with memory that decays as a power law. However, it is not fully clear how such order flow could arise from the predominantly algorithmic market making that characterizes order book dynamics. We show one such consistent mechanism. We treat each market maker as a desk that uses a first-order gradient rule to move the distances of its orders from the mid price toward target distances provided by an unknown oracle. We assume that distinct desks do not act synchronously in time but act whenever new information relevant to their respective oracles arrives, which each desk samples over its own characteristic lookback horizon. Across all desks the rule therefore takes the form of stochastic gradient descent (SGD) with information-driven (mini-batch) noise. In the continuum limit this update rule makes the volume of the order book follow a conservative stochastic partial differential equation (the Dean--Kawasaki equation) whose fluctuations drive the order flow. The distinct horizons of the desks further induce a persistent memory in the order flow that decays as a power law. Standard scaling arguments then lead to rough volatility. We confirm the key predictions of the model on simulated agents and on six months of Binance data with about 790 million orders. In liquid markets governed mainly by market makers, such as the perpetual futures of Binance, the order books are consistent with how our desks change the book.

---

## 295. [DispersedBFT: Bottleneck-Free Primary-Backup Consensus using Dispersed Replication](<https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24833>)

- 作者：Nancy Kansal; Celine Sana; Jelle Hellings
- 发表日期：2026-11-18
- 引用次数：0
- DOI：https://doi.org/10.48550/arxiv.2611.24833
- [打开 PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.24833>)

**摘要：**

The advent of blockchains is fueling the development of novel resilient data management systems (RDSs). Typically, these RDSs are managed using a primary-backup design using a consensus protocol such as PBFT. In these primary-backup designs, a single participant, the primary, is responsible for coordinating the replication and execution of requests. Unfortunately, the central role of the primary makes it a bottleneck that drastically limits performance. In this paper, we propose DispersedBFT, a high performance PBFT-like consensus protocol that utilizes dispersed replication to eliminate the primary bottleneck of PBFT-like consensus. In specific, DispersedBFT uses information dispersal to distribute the task of replicating request from the primary to the backups. We propose several variants of DispersedBFT with either low CPU cost (by using Message Authentication Codes and Merkle proofs) or communication cost (by using digital signatures). We practically analyze the performance of DispersedBFT and show it greatly outperforms PBFT and HotStuff: DispersedBFT supports a throughput of 3.4Mtxn/sec with 43 replicas and is up-to 6.2x faster than PBFT and 2.6x faster than HotStuff.

---
