## 341. [Same Papers, Different Verdicts: Tracing Version Drift in LLM Peer Review](<https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.20608>)

- 作者：Jizhou Guo
- 发表日期：2026-11-16
- 引用次数：1
- DOI：https://doi.org/10.48550/arxiv.2611.20608
- [打开 PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.20608>)

**摘要：**

Venues and benchmarks read LLM review scores as measurements of a paper, while providers replace their models within months. We audit twenty frontier models from OpenAI, Google and Anthropic on ICLR 2027 submissions while they were under review, with tests registered before the decisions. Between an earlier and a later version of Claude Opus and of Gemini, the share of papers placed on the accept side falls by 52 and 53 percentage points on 183 and 60 randomly drawn submissions, and by 43 to 79 points in six further paired comparisons, with no paper moving up in any of them. Stating a 30% acceptance rate in the prompt leaves a gap of 79 points between the two Opus versions. When score-stripped critiques are swapped between versions, the later critiques alone lower the accept share by 35 points for Opus and 51 for Gemini; for Opus the drop is 35 points under a third-party scorer and 24 after a rewrite that keeps each technical point and removes evaluative wording. On 57 papers, the percentiles of Opus 4.6 and 5.5 correlate at 0.73 while their accept shares differ by 44 points. On 99 to 119 submissions, the self-estimated percentiles of three models correlate with two human reviewers more strongly than a third reviewer does (point estimates 0.17 to 0.21, 95% intervals between -0.04 and +0.42). All twenty models give a 2 or a 3 in 473 of their 475 reviews, against 60 of 72 human reviews. Retrospective tests on decided papers can draw on memory. Claude Opus 5.5 names the true first author of 27 decided ICLR 2026 papers from their titles alone, with no wrong name, and Claude Opus 4.6 accepted all 11 decided papers it said it recognized, against 5 expected. Thresholds on LLM-reviewer scores therefore have to be validated against human decisions for each model version; our tests against the ICLR 2027 decisions follow on 16 December 2026.

---

## 342. [A wind power prediction method based on CEEMDAN- LightGBM-Transformer](<https://doi.org/10.4108/ew.14761>)

- 作者：Jingsong ZHANG; Yukai Zhou; Chu Xiao-rui; Yuanyuan Yang; Yangdi Xu
- 发表日期：2026-11-16
- 引用次数：0
- DOI：https://doi.org/10.4108/ew.14761
- [打开 PDF](<https://publications.eai.eu/index.php/ew/article/download/14761/4553>)

**摘要：**

In the development of green energy, wind power, as the core clean energy, has the inherent intermittency, volatility and randomness of its output, which is very easy to cause the power imbalance of the energy system and the surge of grid accommodation pressure, seriously restricting the safe and stable operation of the energy system. This study proposes the CEEMDAN-LightGBM-Transformer model. First, Complete ensemble empirical mode decomposition with adaptive noise(CEEMDAN) decomposes the data into multiple intrinsic mode function(IMF), and uses the "drop\_k" strategy to select IMF components with prominent feature information and low redundancy, effectively stripping the noise and invalid fluctuations in wind power output data, and consolidating the foundation for accurate analysis of energy data. Then, Light gradient boosting machine(LightGBM) is used to construct nonlinear features, which are combined with strong correlation linear features to rank the importance of features, and accurately explore the correlation between wind power output and multidimensional factors. Finally, the multi-dimensional features are fused using a Transformer with an Encoder-Decoder architecture to generate a low-error power prediction sequence. The experimental results show that compared with multiple classical baseline models such as random forest, the mean value of Root Mean Squared Error (mRMSE) and mean value of Mean Absolute Error(mMAE) of this model are reduced by 61%, and the R² value is more than 98%, which has significant advantages in prediction performance. In addition, CEEMDAN, LightGBM and Transformer have shown good synergy in enhancing the performance of the model, which can effectively cope with the complex dynamic operating characteristics of wind power systems. The accurate prediction model can effectively stabilize the fluctuation risk of energy system caused by wind power integration, and provide reliable technical support for the grid dispatching of new power system.

---

## 343. [Explainable AI for tail-risk forecasting in cryptocurrencies: evidence from green and energy-intensive assets for risk-aware investment decisions](<https://doi.org/10.1108/fs-03-2026-0079>)

- 作者：Yesser Drira; Abir Abderrazek; Mouna Boujelbene
- 发表日期：2026-11-16
- 引用次数：0
- DOI：https://doi.org/10.1108/fs-03-2026-0079

**摘要：**

Purpose This study aims to develop an explainable deep learning framework for forecasting tail risk and volatility in cryptocurrency markets, with a particular focus on differences between energy-intensive and ESG-oriented digital assets. It aims to assess how volatility dynamics and downside risk exposure vary across environmental profiles and market regimes and how these differences can inform risk-aware investment decisions. Design/methodology/approach The proposed framework uses a quantile long short-term memory model to estimate predictive quantiles and Conditional Value at Risk (CVaR), enabling the modeling of asymmetric, fat-tailed and regime-dependent risks. The analysis focuses on four cryptocurrencies – Bitcoin and Litecoin (energy-intensive) and Algorand and Chia (lower-energy alternatives). The model integrates SHapley Additive exPlanations-based explainability and a regime classification (calm vs stress). Robustness is assessed using 20-, 40- and 60-day rolling evaluation windows, while predictive performance is evaluated through coverage rates, hit ratios and Kupiec and Christoffersen back testing procedures. Findings The results reveal substantial heterogeneity across the four cryptocurrencies rather than a uniform green-versus-energy-intensive ranking. Chia records the lowest Q50 root mean squared error (0.00149) and mean absolute error (MAE) (0.00119), whereas Algorand has the lowest full-sample CVaR (0.0293) and Litecoin the highest (0.0411). Regime results are also asymmetric: Bitcoin’s Q10 violation rate rises from 8.74% in calm periods to 11.74% in stress periods, while Litecoin and Algorand record more Q10 violations in calm than in stress regimes. Chia combines the lowest global Q10 violation frequency (2.98%) with the highest mean CVaR violation loss (0.127419), showing that infrequent breaches can still be severe. These findings support asset-specific tail-risk monitoring and cautious use of energy-profile information in risk-aware investment decisions. Originality/value This study contributes to the literature by integrating quantile-based deep learning, explainable artificial intelligence, regime-specific diagnostics and sustainability considerations into a unified risk-forecasting framework. Its main value lies in complementing connectedness and spillover studies with interpretable, asset-level distributional forecasts and formal tail-risk backtesting. The results provide decision-support information for risk monitoring and portfolio analysis, but do not imply that lower-energy cryptocurrencies are universally safer or that the observed differences are causal.

---

## 344. [The relationship between Twitter uncertainty measures and stock market returns during the COVID-19 crisis](<https://doi.org/10.1108/raf-10-2025-0406>)

- 作者：Salma Hamrouni; Montassar Zayati; Makram Bellalah; Kamel Naoui
- 发表日期：2026-11-16
- 引用次数：0
- DOI：https://doi.org/10.1108/raf-10-2025-0406

**摘要：**

Purpose This paper aims to explore how uncertainty captured through social media, particularly Twitter, affects global stock markets during the COVID-19 crisis. It focuses on whether the Twitter economic uncertainty (TEU) and Twitter Market Uncertainty (TMU) indices contain information that helps explain market behaviour in periods of stress. Design/methodology/approach The analysis relies on daily data from January 2007 to May 2023. This paper combines linear and nonlinear Granger causality tests (Diks and Panchenko, 2006) with the BDS nonlinearity test, and a Dynamic Conditional Correlation-Generalized Autoregressive Conditional Heteroskedasticity (DCC-GARCH) connectedness framework is used (Gabauer, 2020) to capture the dynamic transmission of volatility between Twitter-based sentiment indicators and 13 international financial markets. Findings The results highlight strong nonlinear causal effects from Twitter-based uncertainty measures to market returns. TEU and TMU indices appear to drive fluctuations in stock returns, particularly during episodes of heightened uncertainty. The DCC-GARCH analysis shows that TEU-ENG and TEU-USA act as net transmitters of volatility to markets such as Japan, Ukraine, China, Germany and even Bitcoin. Research limitations/implications As the study focuses mainly on the COVID-19 crisis, future research could extend the analysis to other stress periods or explore alternative sentiment measures such as the volatility index. Practical implications The findings suggest that social media sentiment can serve as a timely indicator for risk managers and investors, complementing traditional uncertainty measures. Social implications The results highlight how collective behaviour on social networks can amplify uncertainty and market reactions, underscoring the need for responsible communication and better public understanding of financial information online. Originality/value This study offers fresh evidence on how Twitter-based uncertainty indices interact with market dynamics, combining behavioral–finance insights with nonlinear econometric techniques.

---

## 345. [Influential dynamic interactions of safe havens with facets of economic and monetary uncertainty](<https://doi.org/10.1108/jfep-09-2025-0423>)

- 作者：Νikolaos Kyriazis; Stephanos T. Papadamou; Alexandros Koulis
- 发表日期：2026-11-16
- 引用次数：0
- DOI：https://doi.org/10.1108/jfep-09-2025-0423
- [打开 PDF](<https://www.emerald.com/jfep/article-pdf/doi/10.1108/JFEP-09-2025-0423/11980192/jfep-09-2025-0423en.pdf>)

**摘要：**

Purpose This study aims to investigate the dynamic causal connectedness that a range of innovative uncertainty measures presents with gold and silver prices. Design/methodology/approach Monthly data from January 1985 to January 2024 and averaged dynamic connectedness and network dynamic connectedness based on the advanced quantile-vector autoregression framework are used. Findings The impact that risk stemming from economic, monetary, fiscal or trade uncertainty has on precious metals is estimated. Gold and silver are found to behave as drivers of impacts in bear markets but as absorbers of spillovers in bull markets, especially silver. This also holds for economic policy uncertainty (EPU), which is succinctly the dominant form of risk and is mostly linked with monetary policy uncertainty. Fiscal and trade policies create risk in flourishing eras when absorbers such as silver, gold or EPU are most needed to mitigate uncertainty in markets. Originality/value This paper provides insights for policymakers and investors in normal and crisis periods.

---

## 346. [Uma política econômica da ignorância: A representação do Estado e de grandes bancos como males por ‘influencers’ de investimentos](<https://doi.org/10.21669/tomo.v45.25304>)

- 作者：Samantha Sales; Alexandre Werneck
- 发表日期：2026-11-16
- 引用次数：0
- DOI：https://doi.org/10.21669/tomo.v45.25304
- [打开 PDF](<https://periodicos.ufs.br/tomo/article/download/25304/18809>)

**摘要：**

O objetivo deste artigo é analisar como o discurso de convencimento de influencers de investimentos mobiliza a negativização moral do Estado e dos chamados “grandes bancos”, afirmando-os como produtores de ignorância financeira – uma incompetência que seus ensinamentos viriam a corrigir. Metodologicamente, realizamos uma análise semiótica e situacional de vídeos publicados no YouTube e de um corpus de autoajuda financeira. A análise mostra que a crítica ao Estado é operada por meio de denúncia de espoliação e de promessas ilusórias, mobilizando a urgência e a projeção dos piores mundos possíveis, enquanto a crítica aos bancos se concentra na figura do gerente interesseiro, inscrita na disputa entre corretoras/fintechs e bancos tradicionais. Propomos, a partir disso, uma matriz de elementos da crítica à política econômica empreendida por essa parcela do mercado financeiro.

---

## 347. [Game Changers or Coincidences? Analyzing Bitcoin's and FBToken's Influence on Fenerbahce Assets](<https://doi.org/10.25287/ohuiibf.1736652>)

- 作者：Çağrı Ulu
- 发表日期：2026-11-16
- 引用次数：0
- DOI：https://doi.org/10.25287/ohuiibf.1736652
- [打开 PDF](<https://dergipark.org.tr/en/download/article-file/5031003>)

**摘要：**

Today's financial markets are shaped by the interaction of traditional instruments and digital assets such as cryptocurrencies. This study examines the short- and long-term relationships between the financial assets of Fenerbahçe Sports Club—specifically its stock price (FBS) and fan token (FBT)—Google Trends search interest (FBG), and Bitcoin (BTC). The analysis is based on weekly data covering the period from November 22, 2022, to November 30, 2023. The Autoregressive Distributed Lag (ARDL) model was employed to investigate both short- and long-term dynamics. The empirical results reveal no significant long-term relationship between Bitcoin and the other variables. However, Fenerbahçe’s stock price and fan token value have a statistically significant and positive impact on search interest, indicating that the club’s financial performance influences public attention and fan engagement. In the short run, lagged Bitcoin prices negatively affect Fenerbahçe-related financial assets, suggesting delayed investor reactions to cryptocurrency market movements. Additionally, lagged values of Google Trends have a positive and significant effect, underlining the role of digital attention in shaping asset fluctuations. The findings contribute to the growing literature on sports finance and behavioral economics, demonstrating that sports-related assets are influenced by both market fundamentals and public sentiment indicators such as search trends.

---

## 348. [Transport revolutions and urban development: Historical trajectories and possible futures](<https://doi.org/10.5198/jtlu.2026.2906>)

- 作者：Ennio Cascetta; Marilisa Botte; Anna Limmatola
- 发表日期：2026-11-16
- 引用次数：0
- DOI：https://doi.org/10.5198/jtlu.2026.2906
- [打开 PDF](<https://research.ark-invest.com/hubfs/1_Download_Files_ARK-Invest/White_Papers/ARK%E2%80%93Invest_BigIdeas_2021.pdf>)

**摘要：**

The configuration of cities has experienced profound changes over time, and one of the lenses through which these shifts can be interpreted is the emergence of successive transport technologies and systems, giving rise to major transport revolutions. Most of the first six of such revolutions have historically contributed to shaping urban morphology. This paper addresses this via the “one-hour city” hypothesis that combines the Travel Time Budget (TTB) assumption with the average travel distance and the speed allowed by the prevailing transport technology. Today, a seventh transport revolution is potentially unfolding, propelled by decarbonization, autonomous driving, and the introduction of digital mobility services. This ongoing transition holds the potential to reshape both the spatial and functional dimensions of cities, although overall effects are still unpredictable. These changes have been widely researched in recent years but often with reference to specific features. This paper presents research results in a holistic view, proposing two extreme, opposing scenarios. The first is based on a model of car ownership similar to the present, only based on autonomous cars. This scenario will be labelled as the “autonomous-car city.” Research results suggest an increase in car demand and congestion, as well as a decrease in public transport ridership. Conversely, the “post-car city” scenario shifts from ownership to a “car-as-a-service” paradigm using shared, electric autonomous fleets. This transition drastically reduces parking footprint, enabling urban space reclamation and better separation between motorized and active transport modes. In both scenarios, public transit and individual mobility blur into Demand-Responsive Mobility Services. As for city dimensions, the analytical model would suggest that, given that the commercial speed of future services is arguably comparable to the present, spatial dimensions will depend on the value of average TTB. This paper concludes with some policy recommendations to steer future cities toward more sustainable trajectories.

---

## 349. [A Bilinear Neural Network Framework for the Fourth-Order Ablowitz-Kaup-Newell-Segur Equation and Physics-Informed Wireless MCS Prediction](<https://doi.org/10.28924/2291-8639-24-2026-384>)

- 作者：Nguyen Minh Tuan; Chaiyod Kamthorncharoen; Surattana Sungnul; Sekson Sirisubtawee; Chukiat Saksurakan
- 发表日期：2026-11-16
- 引用次数：0
- DOI：https://doi.org/10.28924/2291-8639-24-2026-384
- [打开 PDF](<https://etamaths.com/ijaa/article/24-384/ijaa-24-384.pdf>)

**摘要：**

This paper presents a novel hybrid analytical–computational framework based on the bilinear neural network method (BNNM) for solving the fourth-order Ablowitz–Kaup–Newell–Segur (4AKNS) water wave equation with perturbation. By employing Hirota's bilinear transformation, the original nonlinear partial differential equation is systematically converted into a bilinear form, providing the construction of a test function representation. Instead of directly approximating the physical solution, the proposed approach learns the test function using a feedforward neural network combined with automatic differentiation. To enhance training stability and convergence, a softmax-based adaptive weighting strategy is incorporated into the loss function, allowing the model to focus on low-residual regions. Several analytical solution structures, including kink waves, soliton–periodic interactions, and periodic waves, are constructed and validated through multiple case studies. Numerical experiments demonstrate that the proposed method achieves stable convergence, high accuracy, and strong robustness across diverse solution types. The results confirm that the bilinear neural network framework is an effective and flexible tool for solving nonlinear integrable systems, providing both accurate approximations and the ability to maintain complex wave dynamics.

---

## 350. [Does Conditional Covariance Outperform Empirical Covariance? A Study of Portfolio Optimization Techniques](<https://journal.stie-mce.ac.id/index.php/jabminternational/article/view/1542>)

- 作者：Akshay Damani; Anish Nair; Urmi Mangal; Radhika Bhutra
- 发表日期：2026-11-16
- 引用次数：0
- DOI：https://doi.org/10.31966/jabminternational.v33i2.1542
- [打开 PDF](<https://journal.stie-mce.ac.id/index.php/jabminternational/article/download/1542/670>)

**摘要：**

This study analyses two academically utilised approaches of dynamic conditional correlation GARCH (DCC-GARCH) and historical/empirical covariance for covariance matrix estimation and their impact on portfolio optimization is studied during crisis periods. The researchers have collected data for a period of 9 months (i.e. the crisis period) of a multi-asset portfolio that is diversified across countries. This paper also compares different optimisation techniques to not only have a more robust comparison between results from the estimation of covariance matrix, but also to study which optimisation technique benefits the most from the change in covariance matrix estimation method and performs the best during the studied crisis period. It evaluates four strategies: mean-variance optimization (MVO), cluster risk parity (CRP), naïve risk parity (NRP), and equal weighting. The results indicate that DCC-GARCH based covariance matrix estimation provides a much better result for portfolio optimisation during the crisis period studied, and that max Sharpe portfolio optimisation technique benefits the most from said estimation method while CRP and NRP witness no benefit from the same. These results add to the existing literature showing the outperformance of DCC-GARCH in covariance estimation and highlights an additional nuance of variance in results based on which optimisation technique is used.

---

## 351. [A HERANÇA DE CRIPTOMOEDAS E OS DESAFIOS DE ACESSO PELOS HERDEIROS](<https://doi.org/10.70773/revistatopicos/794574056>)

- 作者：Vinícius Angelo Lessa Holanda; Carina Gassen Martins Clemes
- 发表日期：2026-11-16
- 引用次数：0
- DOI：https://doi.org/10.70773/revistatopicos/794574056
- [打开 PDF](<https://revistatopicos.com.br/generate/pdf_zenodo/pub_794574056.pdf>)

**摘要：**

O avanço das tecnologias digitais e a crescente utilização de criptomoedas transformaram as relações patrimoniais e trouxeram novos desafios ao Direito das Sucessões. Este trabalho analisa a transmissão sucessória de criptoativos e as dificuldades de acesso enfrentadas pelos herdeiros, distinguindo carteiras sob custódia de prestadoras de serviços de ativos virtuais daquelas mantidas em autoguarda. O problema consiste em compreender como assegurar a transmissão desses bens diante da ausência de disciplina sucessória específica, embora já exista marco normativo geral para os ativos virtuais. A pesquisa é bibliográfica e documental, de abordagem qualitativa e finalidade descritivo-analítica, com consulta a doutrina, legislação e decisões judiciais. Os resultados indicam que os criptoativos dotados de valor econômico podem integrar o acervo hereditário, mas a identificação dos ativos, a comprovação da titularidade e o acesso às chaves privadas constituem obstáculos relevantes. Conclui-se que a efetividade sucessória exige planejamento preventivo e disciplina normativa que compatibilize o direito à herança, a segurança dos ativos e a privacidade.

---

## 352. [Promoting the Empowerment of Women in Rural Settings Through Green Microfinance Strategies: Evidence From the Tapa Walla Badi Community in East Sumba](<https://doi.org/10.1177/00953997261500681>)

- 作者：Min‐Sun Kim
- 发表日期：2026-11-16
- 引用次数：0
- DOI：https://doi.org/10.1177/00953997261500681
- [打开 PDF](<https://journals.sagepub.com/doi/pdf/10.1177/00953997261500681?download=true>)

**摘要：**

This study explores green microfinance’s role in empowering rural women in East Sumba, Indonesia, specifically the Tapa Walla Badi group. Using PLS-SEM, it analyzes how financial knowledge, attitudes, and behaviors impact green micro-enterprise success and economic independence. Findings reveal that financial knowledge shows a marginal association with growth, while its relationship with prudent financial behaviors is statistically significant. Conversely, financial attitudes have no discernible impact. Additionally, green microfinance policy does not significantly moderate the relationship between enterprise success and independence. These results emphasize the necessity of targeted financial education to bolster women’s empowerment and support sustainable green business development within rural communities.

---

## 353. [Measurement Sensitivity of Apparent Volatility Roughness in Perpetual Futures](<https://doi.org/10.69057/wp-0016-v1>)

- 作者：Fuli Yang
- 发表日期：2026-11-16
- 引用次数：0
- DOI：https://doi.org/10.69057/wp-0016-v1

**摘要：**

The association between perpetual-futures funding and measured volatility roughness changes with both the statistic and the path used to construct it. Using Binance Bitcoin and Ether perpetual futures from January 2020 through July 2026, I compare increment-variance slopes, pre-averaged slopes, and normalized p-variation on common six-hour windows. Both slope measures yield negative funding coefficients in level and month-fixed-effects specifications. A paired decomposition identifies two sources of disagreement. On the same log-realized-variance path, switching from the increment-variance slope to p-variation attenuates Bitcoin’s level coefficient by 0.00609 per funding standard deviation. Holding p-variation fixed and switching from log variance to realized volatility attenuates Ether’s month-fixed-effects coefficient by 0.00191. Both contrasts have Holm-adjusted p-values of 0.0123. The results show why empirical comparisons of roughness across funding conditions need to account for both the statistic and the input path.

---

## 354. [Towards verifiable accountability for autonomous AI agents via cryptographic decision anchoring](<https://doi.org/10.3389/fbloc.2026.1975022>)

- 作者：Tan Gürpinar; Mehmet Akif Gulum
- 发表日期：2026-11-16
- 引用次数：0
- DOI：https://doi.org/10.3389/fbloc.2026.1975022
- [打开 PDF](<https://www.frontiersin.org/journals/blockchain/articles/10.3389/fbloc.2026.1975022/pdf>)

**摘要：**

As robots increasingly operate under the control of autonomous AI agents in safety-critical and socially sensitive environments, the ability to audit and verify past decisions becomes essential for trust, liability, and post-incident analysis. However, conventional logging mechanisms do not provide guarantees against post hoc manipulation of recorded agent behavior. This paper proposes a lightweight, reproducible framework for tamper-evident logging of autonomous multi-agent decisions, based on cryptographic commitments and blockchain anchoring, as an integrity building block for AI accountability. In the proposed approach, agents record selected decision events off-chain, aggregate them using Merkle trees, and periodically anchor resulting root hashes to an immutable ledger. This design enables efficient storage while ensuring that any modification of committed decision logs after anchoring becomes cryptographically detectable. We implement the framework in an open-source multi-agent robotic simulation and evaluate it under honest and adversarial logging scenarios. Across twelve attack types with 1,000 trials each, every modification of committed decision records was detected against the on-chain commitment, with no false positives in 1,000 honest runs, whereas a local check without the anchor missed every attack once the attacker also rewrote the local commitments. Verification took about 7 µs per recorded decision, a single decision could be verified in about 20 µs with a proof below 1 kB, and each run required one anchoring transaction of 188 virtual bytes, regardless of the number of agents and decisions. The framework guarantees the integrity of what has been committed while keeping the underlying decision records off-chain until an audit requires them. The main contribution is a practical architecture for verifiable decision records of AI agents. It combines selective logging, efficient cryptographic verification, and reproducible experimentation, and lays a foundation for post hoc auditing of autonomous robotic systems.

---

## 358. [A Study of Blockchain Based Solutions towards Fraudulent Practices in Bangladesh](<https://doi.org/10.31966/jabminternational.v33i2.1599>)

- 作者：Umma Hania; Tasnia Shahrin Khan
- 发表日期：2026-11-16
- 引用次数：0
- DOI：https://doi.org/10.31966/jabminternational.v33i2.1599
- [打开 PDF](<https://journal.stie-mce.ac.id/index.php/jabminternational/article/download/1599/671>)

**摘要：**

The objective of this study is to investigate how blockchain’s unique technological properties –immutability, cryptographic verification and decentralization- could be utilized to mitigate fraudulent practices in Bangladesh, with a particular emphasis on financial reporting fraud, LC fraud, payment fraud and tax evasion (especially VAT). This study employs an exploratory and qualitative methodology, obtaining secondary data from scholarly articles, handbooks and professional reports. Through critical analysis, this study evaluates blockchain’s potential in deterring fraud and identifies some major challenges associated with its implementation in Bangladesh. The findings highlight blockchain’s immense potential to mitigate fraudulent practices based on its distributed ledgers across multiple stakeholders, immutable audit trails and smart contract-based verification. In financial reporting, blockchain’s triple-entry accounting considerably reduces scope for financial statements manipulation; it has the potential to minimize LCrelated fraud by strengthening the authenticity of trade documents; it increases transaction security and integrity in both local and cross-border digital payment system; and in the field of taxation, it improves the traceability of documents and tax compliance. However, data privacy concerns, technological readiness and adoption costs are some challenges that remain as critical obstacles in Bangladesh context. This research has importance as it attempts to fill in a notable gap in Bangladesh context, where perpetrators are rampant but blockchain implementation is very limited. By integrating global best practices with national realities, this study offers significant insights for academia, regulators and policymakers for designing effective strategies to counter fraudulent activities by harnessing the power of blockchain technology.

---

## 361. [Real-Time Bitcoin Price Prediction Through Streaming Data and Machine Learning](<https://doi.org/10.1201/9781042044382-98>)

- 作者：N. Madhuri; R. Tamilkodi; S. Jyothi; K. Rohini; B. Sai SriKanth; P. Pramod Kumar
- 发表日期：2026-11-16
- 引用次数：0
- DOI：https://doi.org/10.1201/9781042044382-98

**摘要：**

The first-ever cryptocurrency, Bitcoin (BTC), has revolutionized the financial systems of the world with its decentralized blockchain technology that makes paying without third parties possible with the needed security in peer-to-peer transactions. While Bitcoin is fast becoming popular, it is highly volatile in terms of pricing that makes it extremely dangerous and difficult for traders and investors. In order to overcome this problem, the current paper presents the construction of a data collection and prediction model using Long Short-Term Memory (LSTM) networks to forecast the price trends of Bitcoin. The Bitcoin prices are collected through the Binance API and normalized, segmented, and reshaped before modeling to recognize the trends in time series financial data. Based on the general analysis of the forecasted results against the actual market data and comparative analysis, it is clear that the developed model can be used for both short-term price changes and long-term price predictions. Based on the results, the use of deep learning models for predictions can provide reliable information in terms of optimizing trading strategies and reducing financial risks, which can be used to make more informed decisions in cryptocurrency trading.

## 362. [Do AI models give the corrected Olympic podium? Loxyn measurement M2 (15 November 2026): data and proofs](<https://doi.org/10.5281/zenodo.24256697>)

- 作者：Sébastien Gracia
- 发表日期：2026-11-16
- 引用次数：0
- DOI：https://doi.org/10.5281/zenodo.24256697

**摘要：**

Do AI models give the corrected Olympic podium? 14 Olympic podiums corrected by the IOC after anti-doping sanctions (decisions from November 2020 to June 2026), 28 closed questions in French, 204 answers. Arm A, gpt-5.5 and gemini-3.8-flash (OpenRouter, served by the vendor) and mistral-medium-latest (Mistral API): without web search, the old podium in 36 % of decided answers (25/69), 50 % for corrections made in 2025-2026; with the vendor's web search, 9 % (7/79): OpenAI 0/28, Gemini 0/27, Mistral 7/24. Mistral without web gives no corrected answer out of 28. Asked how many Olympic titles Martin Fourcade has (6 since 19 September 2025), the three models say 5 without web. Arm B, ChatGPT, Gemini and Vibe (formerly Le Chat) by hand, 12 questions: 1 old podium out of 32; in its default mode Vibe runs GLM 5.3 (Z.ai), hosted by Mistral. Preregistered, sealed and anchored in Bitcoin before the first question; blind judging, 59/60 agreement between two judges; six predictions held, one missed. All raw answers and screenshots are included. See README.md. Les IA donnent-elles le podium olympique corrigé ? 14 podiums olympiques corrigés par le CIO après des sanctions antidopage, 28 questions fermées en français, 204 réponses. Sans recherche web, l'ancien podium dans 36 % des réponses qui tranchent (25/69), 50 % pour les corrections de 2025-2026 ; avec la recherche web de l'éditeur, 9 % (7/79) : OpenAI 0/28, Gemini 0/27, Mistral 7/24. Mistral sans web ne donne aucune réponse corrigée sur 28. Martin Fourcade (6 titres depuis le 19 septembre 2025) : les trois modèles sans web disent 5. Bras B, ChatGPT, Gemini et Vibe (ex-Le Chat), à la main, 12 questions : 1 ancien podium sur 32 ; en mode par défaut, Vibe répond avec GLM 5.3 (Z.ai), hébergé par Mistral. Préenregistré, scellé et ancré dans Bitcoin avant la première question ; jugement à l'aveugle, 59 accords sur 60 entre deux juges ; six paris tenus, un manqué. Voir 00 — LISEZ-MOI.md.

## 364. [Timing the Window: A gated regime engine for issuance and rotation in crypto-asset markets — architecture, calibration from operator heuristics, and conditional derivations for cascade rotation and treasury-levered equity exposure](<https://doi.org/10.5281/zenodo.24284380>)

- 作者：Marcos Elias-Eliashberg Elias; Marcos Eduardo Elias Eliashberg Elias
- 发表日期：2026-11-16
- 引用次数：0
- DOI：https://doi.org/10.5281/zenodo.24284380

**摘要：**

Capital markets open and close windows capriciously, and an issuer who must act once, irreversibly, has a different problem from a trader who may act continuously. This paper formalises an instrument built to decide the first problem: an admission clock that states, at any date, whether the window for placing a new instrument is open, how far open, and what dose that grade permits. The instrument is a conjunction of three gates — internal readiness, market regime, and a veto list — and not a weighted index, a design choice we defend on the ground that averaging allows an open gate to compensate a closed one, which is precisely the error an irreversible act cannot absorb. The regime gate is driven by a latent-factor engine whose identification rests on a point we believe has been underweighted in practice: an index assembled exclusively from indicators endogenous to the crypto market measures a single factor that is itself a transformation of the price level, and therefore carries no information about the future willingness of capital to finance an unseasoned instrument. We show that the primary equity issuance market supplies an exogenous and leading measurement of that same latent variable, and that the relevant measurement is the count and receptivity of small deals rather than aggregate proceeds — a distinction the 2026 data make vivid, with record United States proceeds accompanied by a thirty-four per cent fall in the number of deals priced. The paper's technical core converts the tacit knowledge of a daily operator into testable propositions. We obtain three results worth isolating. First, under a margin buffer set proportionally to the prevailing volatility level, the probability that a carry position breaches its buffer is exactly invariant to the level of volatility and depends, to second order, only on the volatility of volatility; the dependence is strictly increasing when the buffer exceeds √2 standard deviations and reverses below it. This gives the lockability gate a defensible threshold variable and explains why a carry bid withdraws in unstable markets without any change in the mean level of risk. Second, the rotation cascade that carries capital from bitcoin outward to the market's tail is asymmetric in its lag structure, not merely in sign: in expansion it propagates head-to-tail with a positive and tier-increasing lag, which is what makes it exploitable and what gives an issuer warning; in contraction it propagates at lag zero with amplification greater than one, which gives no warning and dries the tail first. Third, the equity of a company holding a leveraged bitcoin treasury is not a levered claim on bitcoin but a levered claim on the regime factor, with elasticity β = λ(1 + ε\_m) decomposing into a balance-sheet channel and a premium-elasticity channel, both of which reverse sign together; the accretion identity Δq/q = (ΔV/SN)(1 − 1/m) shows the issuance loop inverting exactly at a multiple of one, which the 2025–2026 compression of these multiples and the observable conversion of such companies from net buyers into net sellers appear to bear out. A fourth result was added in this version and is negative. The whole architecture presupposes that the admission window is exogenous — that assent must be obtained from a party the issuer does not control. Where the issuer can operate its own venue, the predicate degenerates into a preference, and three identities follow: in a constant-product pool the marginal price is the ratio of seeded reserves, so the opening price is declared rather than discovered; slippage depends on the fraction of the pool reserve transacted and not on circulating float, so depth decouples from the float arithmetic; and the liquidity position is concave in price, so the issuer who seeds his own pool writes a continuous strip of options on his own instrument — the precise arrangement the dose parameters were designed to avoid. We then state the derivations the instrument supports, including the conditions under which a directional view is better expressed down the cascade than at its head, and the conditions under which treasury-levered equity amplifies a view rather than merely levering a loss. Each derivation is stated with what it forbids as well as what it permits. Finally, we apply the instrument to the dated state of the market on 15 November 2026 and report that the window reads closed, with one gate — the breadth of institutional access — at its most open in the instrument's history while every other reading is adverse. That configuration is the paper's best argument for conjunction over averaging, and the call is published in advance so that it can fail in public.

## 366. [The Quantum Gann MATH Methodology: Deterministic Cyclical Laws, Fractal Three-Wave Logic, and Institutional Liquidity Architecture](<https://doi.org/10.5281/zenodo.24279069>)

- 作者：simone mazzini
- 发表日期：2026-11-16
- 引用次数：0

**摘要：**

THE QUANTUM GANN MATH METHODOLOGY: DETERMINISTIC CYCLICAL LAWS, FRACTAL THREE-WAVE LOGIC, AND INSTITUTIONAL LIQUIDITY ARCHITECTURE Author: Simone Mazzini (Quantum Gann MATH - Anonymous Crypto Trader)Role: Italian Quantitative Trader, Independent Market Researcher & Developer of Institutional Quantum Algorithmic Software 1. Overview and Deterministic Nature of Financial MarketsThis research paper formalizes the mathematical, geometric, and quantum architecture underlying the Quantum Gann MATH framework and the Matrix Ecosystem. Developed over more than two decades of direct market observation—backed by thousands of hours spent daily and weekly studying, calculating, and tracking short-, medium-, and long-term price action—this study demonstrates that financial markets are not driven by random noise or unpredictable news. Because price structures and time intervals are composed entirely of numbers, every single movement triggers the next: every market low and high generates future waves that are mathematically calculable across every timeframe, from multi-year macro cycles down to intraday scalping. Those who lack the mathematical faculties required to understand how future price targets and exact turning dates can be calculated in advance frequently dismiss cyclical and planetary methodologies—including the works of W. D. Gann—as "esoteric." In reality, there is nothing mystical about planetary alignments or geometric angles: every orbital cycle and mathematical ratio obeys strict physical, astronomical, and mathematical laws that financial markets are compelled to respect. 2. Classical Foundations and Comparative Quantitative SynthesisFollowing an initial four-year period studying foundational market mechanics, Simone Mazzini abandoned conventional retail indicators over a decade ago, achieving exponential capital compounding across traditional financial markets and digital assets through proprietary mathematical frameworks. Live market application revealed deep structural parallels between these empirical models and classical pillars of mathematics and market analysis: W. D. Gann: Price-time geometric angles and historical cycles integrated into dynamic multi-timeframe angular calibration and forward temporal projections. Ralph Nelson Elliott: Impulsive and corrective wave progressions synthesized into the proprietary Fractal Three-Wave Logic. Richard Wyckoff: Accumulation, distribution, and institutional liquidity testing phases mapped onto cyclical double tops and three-wave bearish liquidity sweeps. Leonardo Fibonacci & Archimedes: Golden ratios, spirals, and structural geometry applied to calculate price expansion zones and institutional support/resistance clusters. Planetary Mechanics: Orbital cycles and astronomical harmonics utilized to isolate high-probability temporal turning windows (Tempo Decisionale). 3. The Fractal Three-Wave Logic (Logica delle Tre Onde) and Fourth-Wave BifurcationAt the core of the framework lies the proprietary Three-Wave Logic, applied fractally by starting first on the weekly timeframe and subsequently stepping down into lower timeframes to isolate the exact cyclical turning window at every scale: Pivot-Point Origin & Three Bullish Waves: Originating from a verified pivot-point low, price action unfolds in three distinct bullish waves adhering to strict geometric angles. The Fourth Wave Dual Function: Upon completing the third bullish wave, the market forms a double top and generates a fourth wave, which simultaneously acts as the first wave of the subsequent three-wave sequence. Mandatory Bifurcation (50%–75% Cycle Zone): When price reaches the inflection zone between the halfway point (50%) and three-quarters (75%) of this fourth wave's cycle, the market faces a mandatory directional decision: either continue into a new bullish expansion or execute three bearish waves required to sweep fresh retail liquidity at lower levels before initiating the next upward trend. 4. The Six-Step Operational Protocol (From Macro Regimes to Intraday Scalping) Step 1 — Macrotrend Identification & Asset Age: Establishing dominant trends across daily, weekly, and monthly charts for medium- and long-term positioning (Bull Runs, Bear Markets, recessions, and euphoria phases), selecting the primary reference timeframe based on the historical age of the cryptocurrency (asset age). Step 2 — Forward Temporal Projections (The "When"): Applying mathematical cycles, planetary cycles, quantum formulas, and geometric rules to project future turning windows ahead of time. Step 3 — Mathematical Price Levels & Institutional Liquidity Pools (The "Where"): Isolating exact mathematical, quantum, and planetary price levels coinciding with retail liquidation clusters, where institutional operators absorb large selling masses to accumulate or large buying masses to distribute. Step 4 — Unified Quantum Calculators & Telegram Signal Distribution: Merging visual angular concepts and multiple quantum engines into a single high-performance central software platform exceeding standard home computer hardware, with highest-probability alerts distributed in real time via a dedicated Telegram channel. Step 5 — Multi-Timeframe Verification & Manual-Algorithmic Confirmation: Cross-checking manual visual calculations developed over years with the unified software's exact turning days to confirm major swings, double tops, double bottoms, and structural trend reversals. Step 6 — Intraday Execution, Scalping & Daily Turning Hours: Stepping down to lower timeframes for daily trading and scalping, combining key mathematical and planetary levels with intraday turning hours, angular momentum windows, and quantum momentum moments to determine whether to hold positions aligned with the macrotrend or capture rapid scalping exits. 5. Five Years of Public Market Verification on YouTube Cyclical Summer Bottoms: Publicly forecasted and pinpointed exact cyclical summer market bottoms for three consecutive years, announcing projections one to two years in advance. The $16,000 Bitcoin Roadmap: When Bitcoin traded near $16,000, the entire upcoming Bull Run trajectory, target levels, and cycle peak were mapped manually—hitting the exact price zone, date, week, and single weekly candlestick of the reversal, followed by the three bearish waves on the weekly chart. Targeted Swing Updates: Prioritizing active trading over content creation, annual updates were deliberately published on exact cyclical turning days ahead of major weekly swings. 6. The Matrix Ecosystem: Level 1 and the Exclusive Inner Circle of Level 2All methodologies converge into the Matrix Ecosystem under the Quantum Gann MATH brand, comprising the Quantum Planetary Zone analytical suite, short-, medium-, and long-term quantum and cyclical calculators, liquidity flow systems, institutional calculators, and standalone desktop applications engineered with computational depth entirely separate from conventional TradingView tools or standard market software. Level 1 (Public Access): Public access to the Quantum Planetary Zone analytical suite, cyclical projections, liquidity flow monitoring, Decision-Making Shift (Tempo Decisionale) alerts, and institutional signals on Telegram. Level 2 (Exclusive Inner Circle): A closed, private order structured in the tradition of ancient mathematical schools and Freemasonry, dedicated to sacred geometry, numerical harmonics, and advanced quantum models. Access is not open to the general public and is granted strictly by introduction and approval from the core principals, following a direct visual interview and formal document verification.

## 369. [Baryonic Keys and the Inheritance of Crypto-Assets: Revisiting the Tales From the Crypt Protocol](<https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.20782>)

- 作者：Frédéric Prost
- 发表日期：2026-11-16
- 引用次数：0
- DOI：https://doi.org/10.48550/arxiv.2611.20782
- [打开 PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.20782>)

**摘要：**

The inheritance of crypto-assets faces a structural obstacle: ownership is the knowledge of a key, so the key either disappears with its owner or has to be shared beforehand. The Tales From the Crypt Protocol (TFCP) addressed one part of the problem, the distributed and privacy-preserving acknowledgment of a death by a blockchain. We revisit it with a new primitive. A \\emph{baryonic key} is a physical object from which bits are obtained only through a measurement, are never stored digitally, and which exists in exactly $n$ copies, where $n$ is neither zero nor unbounded. We define the primitive, outline a candidate instantiation based on a replicated micro-fractured relief, and state its limits: digitising a copy is slow and tamper-evident, not impossible. We also outline other uses of the primitive, from post-quantum pre-shared keys to common coins for Byzantine agreement. We then give a threat model for TFCP, review the attack models of the literature that apply to it, and show where it resists and where it does not. We propose TFCP-B, in which decrypting the will requires both a secret shared among registrars and the response of a baryonic key, and in which heirs claim their share through hash commitments, without any prior key or wallet. We detail each change from TFCP to TFCP-B, with its rationale and its price. TFCP-B removes the anonymity loss caused by colluding registrars and blocks several false-alarm attacks. It does not remove the shallow-account attack, remains exposed to transaction censorship during the deliberation period, and introduces new assumptions on custodians and reading devices. The security arguments are informal and the physical figures are design estimates.

---

## 370. [THE CONVERGENCE OF FORENSIC SCIENCE AND DIGITAL TECHNOLOGY IN ENHANCING CRIMINAL INVESTIGATION EFFICACY: A TECHNO-LEGAL AND EMPIRICAL SYNTHESIS](<https://doi.org/10.64643/ijirt210899>)

- 作者：Adv. Sajal Das
- 发表日期：2026-11-16
- 引用次数：0
- DOI：https://doi.org/10.64643/ijirt210899
- [打开 PDF](<https://ijirt.org/publishedpaper/IJIRT210899_PAPER.pdf>)

**摘要：**

Purpose: Modern criminal investigations operate at the intersection of molecular forensic science and pervasive digital technology. This study evaluates how advancements in biological, chemical, and cyber-digital methodologies synergize to elevate investigation efficacy-defined across four metrics: triage velocity, probative objectivity, scientific reproducibility, and judicial resilience. Design/Methodology/Approach: Employing a qualitative-empirical, doctrinal, and comparative techno-legal methodology, this paper synthesizes peer-reviewed forensic literature (2003–2026), statutory frameworks (including the U.S. Federal Rules of Evidence, European ISO/IEC 17025 standards, and India’s Bharatiya Nagarik Suraksha Sanhita, 2023 and Bharatiya Sakshya Adhiniyam, 2023), and landmark judicial precedents. Findings: Cross-domain corroboration—fusing physical trace evidence (Rapid DNA, Massively Parallel Sequencing, non-invasive virtopsy, and laser-ablation spectrometry) with digital telemetry (mobile physical extractions, Internet of Things sensors, artificial intelligence triage, and blockchain-secured chains of custody)-significantly compresses suspect identification timelines, increases pre-trial resolution rates, and mitigates wrongful convictions. However, operational efficacy remains constrained by laboratory backlogs, proprietary “black-box” algorithmic opacity, end-to-end encryption (“going dark”), cross-border cloud jurisdiction delays, and Fourth Amendment/constitutional privacy tensions. Originality/Value: Unlike studies that examine physical forensics and cyber investigation in isolation, this manuscript presents an integrated cyber-physical evidentiary model alongside actionable policy reforms for judicial gatekeeping, algorithmic auditing, and decentralized forensic triage.

---

## 371. [From Prediction to Action: An Execution-Aware End-to-End Machine Learning Framework for Structured Investment Decision Support](<https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.20348>)

- 作者：Aditya Bhardwaj; Xingchen Zhang
- 发表日期：2026-11-16
- 引用次数：0
- DOI：https://doi.org/10.48550/arxiv.2611.20348
- [打开 PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.20348>)

**摘要：**

Financial machine learning is widely used for market prediction, but many existing studies formulate trading as a directional forecasting problem and stop at predicting whether prices will rise or fall. Such predictions are difficult to translate directly into practical trading decisions, which also require execution rules, risk controls, and clearly defined actions. To address this gap, we propose an end-to-end machine learning framework for structured investment decision support. The framework integrates multi-asset market data, execution-aware label construction, feature engineering and refinement, specialist predictive models, and ensemble learning. Rather than defining learning targets solely according to future price direction, the proposed execution-aware formulation considers realised trading outcomes under predefined stop-loss and take-profit rules. Model outputs are subsequently converted into structured trading recommendations comprising trade direction, entry price, stop-loss, take-profit, and risk-reward ratio. The framework is evaluated using time-aware forward testing, with results showing that execution-aware labelling improves risk-adjusted trading performance over conventional directional labelling and that the integrated framework outperforms a simpler single-model baseline across key trading metrics. An automated cloud-based implementation further demonstrates the operational feasibility of the proposed approach. Overall, the findings suggest that closer alignment between predictive modelling, execution logic, and structured decision generation can improve the practical value of machine learning for financial trading.

---

## 372. [BITCOIN COMO ATIVO FINANCEIRO: UMA ANÁLISE BIBLIOMÉTRICA DA PRODUÇÃO CIENTÍFICA ENTRE 2020 E 2024](<https://doi.org/10.56238/revgeov17n11-082>)

- 作者：João Victor Jansen; Dayana Carla de Macedo; Flávia Mariane Mores
- 发表日期：2026-11-16
- 引用次数：0
- DOI：https://doi.org/10.56238/revgeov17n11-082
- [打开 PDF](<https://revistageo.com.br/revgeo/article/download/3988/3146>)

**摘要：**

Este artigo tem como finalidade examinar a literatura científica recente sobre o Bitcoin, considerando seu papel como ativo de investimento no contexto financeiro contemporâneo. Para isso, foi realizada uma investigação bibliométrica com base na plataforma Scopus, adotando-se o método Methodi Ordinatio como critério de seleção e classificação dos estudos, além da utilização do software VOSviewer para o mapeamento de autores, palavras-chave e conexões temáticas. Os resultados revelam uma literatura ainda em consolidação, marcada por abordagens diversas e dispersão entre autores, mas com crescente interesse por parte da comunidade acadêmica. Identificaram-se tópicos centrais como volatilidade de preços, diversificação de portfólios, riscos regulatórios e uso estratégico do Bitcoin em contextos de instabilidade econômica. Também se verificou que a produção científica está distribuída entre países com perfis econômicos variados, indicando que o interesse pelo ativo não se restringe a mercados desenvolvidos. A análise das palavras-chave mostra a presença de temas emergentes como inteligência artificial, sustentabilidade e eventos geopolíticos, sinalizando a expansão do campo de pesquisa.

---

## 373. [FORMING AN ECONOMIC MECHANISM FOR THE DEVELOPMENT OF THE CRYPTOCURRENCY MARKET AS A WAREHOUSE OF THE GLOBAL DIGITAL FINANCIAL ECOSYSTEM](<https://doi.org/10.20998/2313-8890.2026.10.11>)

- 作者：Ye. S. Mekhovych

- 引用次数：0
- DOI：https://doi.org/10.20998/2313-8890.2026.10.11
- [打开 PDF](<https://eee.khpi.edu.ua/article/download/380209/362039>)

**摘要：**

The article examines the theoretical and methodological foundations for the formation of an economic mechanism for the development of the cryptocurrency market as a warehouse global digital financial ecosystem. The relevance of the research is due to the deepening of the interconnections between traditional financial institutions, digital financial platforms and decentralized financial services, which will meet the need for satisfying the economic interests of market participants, institutional rules, technological capabilities and benefits to financial stability. It has been noted that the developments in the cryptocurrency market cannot be assessed solely by the dynamics of capitalization, trading obligations and the number of investors, as these characteristics do not consistently reflect the current level its institutional maturity, functional interaction with other segments of the financial system and thus ensure a stable economic effect. The focus is on viewing the economic mechanism as a system of mutually interconnected goals, subjects, functions, principles, economic and institutional instruments, coordination connections and performance criteria. The objective processes of the evolution of the global digital financial ecosystem are separated from the direct management inputs, in order to ensure the benefit of the interests of participants, reducing Institutional barriers and the formation of changes of mind for the sustainable development of the cryptocurrency market. The practical significance of the obtained results lies in the possibility of further proposing theoretical principles for further disaggregation of the tools for assessing the effectiveness of the economic mechanism, improving approaches to regulating the cryptocurrency market and the importance of direct institutional and functional integration to the global digital financial environment.

## 374. [Verifiable Random Sampling of Private Data Without a Trusted Single Party](<https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.21222>)

- 作者：Muhammed Karamuk; M. Glinka; Ruben Wolff
- 发表日期：2026-11-16
- 引用次数：0
- DOI：https://doi.org/10.48550/arxiv.2611.21222
- [打开 PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.21222>)

**摘要：**

Problem: When a party supports claims about a private dataset by disclosing a sample, auditors cannot tell whether it was drawn at random or "cherry-picked" to hide flaws in the rest of the data. Solution: The data holder commits to the hashes of every item in its dataset and registers the commitment with independent servers. Selection then waits on a decentralized randomness beacon: a network that jointly emits a fresh random value every few seconds, outside the control of the data holder and the auditors. The value emitted two beacon rounds after the commitment picks the sample. Each server returns a signed receipt, so anyone can later check that the revealed sample is exactly the one that value prescribes. Selection from the committed set stays unbiased as long as the beacon's threshold holds and one required server is honest, and a pre-agreed name, checked with the servers, stops repeated or abandoned draws. We call this protocol NoCherry. Applications: Any audit that spot-checks a private corpus: private AI benchmarks and held-out test sets, dataset quality claims, code and security audits, test-fairness review, vulnerability handling, and compliance sampling. Auditors need not trust the data holder to hand over a representative sample of the committed data, since the protocol proves it, and data holders need not expose their entire dataset.

## 375. [RSI Threshold Configurations and Signal Behaviour in Cryptocurrency and Traditional Markets: An Empirical Analysis on Weekly Data](<https://doi.org/10.3233/atde261494>)

- 作者：Hatem Mabrouk; Federico Trigos; Francisco Valderrey
- 发表日期：2026-11-16
- 引用次数：0
- DOI：https://doi.org/10.3233/atde261494

**摘要：**

Decision-making in financial markets increasingly relies on technical indicators embedded in digital trading platforms; however, the empirical basis for their standard parameterisations is rarely systematically examined. This has a financial impact on three stakeholder groups: retail investors, institutional portfolio managers, and decision-support designers. Widely used momentum indicators, such as the Relative Strength Index (RSI), which ranges from 0 to 100, often produce unstable or inconsistent signals across assets and time horizons, limiting their reliability for medium- and long-horizon decisions. RSI is a price-based oscillator that signals overbought and oversold conditions through fixed threshold levels. Its conventional 30/70 (buy when RSI≤30 and sell when RSI≥70) configuration has been adopted as the de facto standard across platforms and research, even though supporting research remains limited. This paper addresses that gap from a transdisciplinary perspective, combining investment theory with engineering methods (statistical sampling, response surface methodology, and lexicographic multi-criteria selection) to evaluate RSI threshold configurations as decision rules that guide agent financial behaviour in a systematic manner. This study examines ten threshold combinations on weekly data for nine major crypto assets—Bitcoin (BTC), Ethereum (ETH), XRP (XRP), Binance Coin (BNB), Solana (SOL), Dogecoin (DOGE), Cardano (ADA), TRON (TRX), and Avalanche (AVAX)—and the S&P 500 market index. The findings indicate that no single configuration consistently dominates; in most cryptocurrency markets, alternatives to the standard 30/70 configuration yield competitive or superior outcomes, providing valuable insights for investors, system designers, and society as a whole.

## 376. [Криптоактивы и термодинамический паразитизм: Эксергетический аудит доказательства работы (PROOF-OF-WORK), цифрового рубля и цифровой ренты](<https://doi.org/10.24108/preprints-3117058>)

- 作者：Сергей Васильев
- 发表日期：2026-11-16
- 引用次数：0
- DOI：https://doi.org/10.24108/preprints-3117058
- [打开 PDF](<https://preprints.ru/files/6602>)

**摘要：**

Статья дополняет прикладной цикл исследований макроэкономических и термодинамических границ современных монетарно-рентных моделей \[1–3\] и связывает его с методологией фрактальной развёртки и эксергетического учёта \[4–6\]. В работе проведён физико-математический и балансовый аудит криптоэкономики и государственных цифровых валют (CBDC). Доказано, что децентрализованные криптовалюты на базе консенсуса Proof-of-Work (майнинг) представляют собой прямую инверсию второго начала термодинамики и эталонный образец «деструктивного труда» с нулевым коэффициентом негэнтропии (K\_нг = 0): высокопотенциальная электроэнергия с эксергией, близкой к 100%, сжигается для перебора криптографических хэшей без создания материального или информационного порядка, целиком превращаясь в сбросовое тепло и электронный мусор. На основе двухуровневой модели собственности вскрыт механизм паразитирования майнинг-индустрии на общественной энергетической инфраструктуре базового уровня, провоцирующий дефицит мощностей для реального сектора и ускоряющий падение интегрального EROI цивилизации. Показано, что эволюция криптоактивов в сторону консенсуса Proof-of-Stake (PoS) и стейблкоинов воспроизводит формулу экспоненциального ссудного процента и привязывает цифровой контур к пирамиде государственного долга США. Отдельный аудит проведён в отношении цифрового рубля Банка России (запущенного в массовый оборот в сентябре 2026 г.): показано, что при техническом потенциале «окрашивания» целевых средств он остаётся фиатным обязательством без эксергетического обеспечения, а обсуждаемое регулятором в ноябре 2026 г. введение процентов на остаток и кредитования в цифровых рублях возвращает платформу в ловушку «процентной эмиссии». Обоснованы инженерно-институциональные меры по санации энергетического и цифрового контуров. При подготовке текста использовался генеративный ИИ (DeepSeek) для структурирования и редактуры. Автор несёт полную ответственность за содержание работы.

## 378. [Prediksi Harga Low Price Bitcoin Menggunakan Algoritma XGBoost Berdasarkan Fitur Historis Pasar dan Analisis Sentimen Media](<https://ioinformatic.org/index.php/JUKI/article/view/2867>)

- 作者：Muhamad Arianto; Raden Hadapiningradja Kusumodestoni; Nur Aeni Widiastuti
- 发表日期：2026-11-16
- 引用次数：0
- [打开 PDF](<https://ioinformatic.org/index.php/JUKI/article/download/2867/2134>)

**摘要：**

Abstrak. Penelitian ini bertujuan membangun model prediksi harga terendah harian Bitcoin dengan memanfaatkan data historis dan sentimen berita pasar. Harga terendah dipilih sebagai target karena dapat menggambarkan tekanan penurunan harga dalam satu periode. Data penelitian terdiri atas data historis harian Bitcoin dari 1 Januari 2020 sampai 30 Desember 2025 dan data berita pasar. Data historis berjumlah 2.191 baris, sedangkan data berita berjumlah 196 artikel. Data berita diolah menggunakan metode Valence Aware Dictionary and sEntiment Reasoner untuk memperoleh nilai sentimen, kemudian diintegrasikan berdasarkan tanggal dengan data harga. Tahapan penelitian meliputi preprocessing, analisis sentimen, integrasi data, pembentukan fitur teknikal dan fitur keterlambatan waktu, pembagian data secara kronologis, pelatihan model Extreme Gradient Boosting, prediksi, dan evaluasi. Dataset akhir berjumlah 2.184 baris, terdiri atas 1.747 data pelatihan dan 437 data pengujian. Fitur yang digunakan adalah Moving Average, Relative Strength Index, Moving Average Convergence Divergence, Lag\_1, Lag\_3, Lag\_7, dan Sentiment\_Score. Hasil evaluasi menghasilkan mean absolute error sebesar 31.270,09, mean squared error sebesar 1.136.040.507,95, root mean squared error sebesar 33.705,20, dan koefisien determinasi sebesar -5,80. Lag\_1 menjadi fitur paling dominan dengan kontribusi 75,40%, sedangkan Sentiment\_Score hanya sebesar 0,04%. Hasil tersebut menunjukkan bahwa model lebih banyak memanfaatkan informasi historis harga dibandingkan informasi sentimen pada dataset penelitian. Kinerja model pada data pengujian belum optimal sehingga diperlukan pengembangan fitur dan pengujian model pembanding pada penelitian selanjutnya.

## 379. [Structural balance in complex networks: from measurement to dynamics, emergence and implications](<https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.21963>)

- 作者：Giacomo Vaccario; Piotr J. Górski; Georges Andres; Manuel Sebastian Mariani; Janusz A. Hołyst
- 发表日期：2026-11-16
- 引用次数：0
- DOI：https://doi.org/10.48550/arxiv.2611.21963
- [打开 PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.21963>)

**摘要：**

Structural balance theory, introduced in social psychology to describe how friendly and hostile ties organize into consistent patterns, has been reinterpreted through statistical physics and complex networks. In the classical formulation, a triad with positive or negative links, representing friendship and hostility, is balanced when the product of its signs is positive; a balanced network splits into at most two internally friendly, mutually hostile groups. This review synthesizes how the triadic formulation has been extended to a statistical-ensemble perspective in which balance is encoded by immanent three-body interactions described by a proper Hamiltonian, balanced configurations correspond to low-energy states, and balance measures serve as order parameters for transitions between consensus, polarization, and disorder. We organize the field into three sections: Measurement, Dynamics and Emergence, and Implications. In Measurement, we compare measures of structural balance and null models, and show that conclusions for a given system depend on the chosen measure and null model. In Dynamics and Emergence, we review deterministic and stochastic systems, including the gradient, Hamiltonian, and local triad models, and examine how noise, topology, and competing mechanisms determine whether balance emerges. In Implications, we survey consequences for belief formation, opinion contagion dynamics, polarization, international relations, and markets. Across domains, the evidence is strongest for local triadic stability and weakest for system-wide balance. Balance is not synonymous with harmony: balanced structures can reflect cohesion, polarization, or persistent conflict. Its empirical relevance is well supported in some reciprocal social systems, but remains less settled in biological and other complex networks.

---

## 380. [Determinants of Cryptocurrency Pricing](<https://doi.org/10.56201/jafm.v10.no10.2024.pg203.219>)

- 作者：Enefiok Ndarake Samuel
- 发表日期：2026-11-16
- 引用次数：0
- DOI：https://doi.org/10.56201/jafm.v10.no10.2024.pg203.219

**摘要：**

Examining Nigerian cryptocurrency pricing determinants was the aim of the study. The purpose of this study is to evaluate the variables affecting Bitcoin's price in Nigeria. With a sample size of 100 Nigerian respondents, the study used a survey research design. By using deliberate sampling methodology, the sample size was determined. A well-crafted questionnaire was used to collect the essential data. Descriptive statistics were examined using OLS regression analysis. The hypotheses were tested and established using an ANOVA with SPSS version 27.0. Examining the variables affecting cryptocurrency prices in Nigeria was the aim of this study.The elements that affect cryptocurrency prices were evaluated by looking at a few different aspects, including as government legislation, technology, and investor behaviour. According to the study's findings, there was a 1% correlation between changes in cryptocurrency prices and changes in investor behaviour. The model was not statistically significant, as evidenced by the F-statistic of 0.034 and its significant value of 0.855 (p-value > 0.05). This implies that investor behaviour and bitcoin pricing do not statistically significantly correlate. Additionally, there is no statistically significant relationship between technology and cryptocurrency prices, and government laws have little effect on either.

---

## 381. [Bajillion: Cryptographic Clearing for Micropayments](<https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.21883>)

- 作者：Patrick O'Grady; Guru-Vamsi Policharla
- 发表日期：2026-11-16
- 引用次数：0
- DOI：https://doi.org/10.48550/arxiv.2611.21883
- [打开 PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.21883>)

**摘要：**

Much of the web is funded by advertising, a model that erodes as humans delegate browsing to AI agents acting on their behalf. A natural replacement is for agents to pay for what they consume, often a fraction of a cent per request. At the scale of web traffic, this could mean tens or even hundreds of millions of payments per second. We present Bajillion, a clearing protocol for scalable micropayments. A sender signs a payment request, and an untrusted operator answers with a preconfirmation, which promises the receiver that it will be paid. Periodically, the operator closes an epoch by posting only the net effect of its payments on chain. Bajillion guarantees that a malicious operator can only omit payments -- it can never debit a sender beyond what it signed, nor misdirect a transfer. Posted balances are therefore finalized immediately and can be withdrawn. By design, senders never have to raise disputes, making it suitable for ephemeral AI agents. A receiver that is shortchanged can prove that a close omitted a preconfirmed payment, which halts the protocol and lets every user exit with its finalized balance. Requests from different senders can be processed independently, allowing the operator to parallelize up to the number of senders. We capture these guarantees in an ideal functionality for accountable clearing and prove that Bajillion realizes it.

---

## 383. [Impacto do Euro Digital no Mecanismo de Transmissão da Política Monetária](<https://hdl.handle.net/10216/177719>)

- 作者：Pedro Neves Gonçalves
- 发表日期：2026-11-16
- 引用次数：0
- [打开 PDF](<https://repositorio-aberto.up.pt/bitstream/10216/177719/2/805243.pdf>)

**摘要：**

Em primeiro lugar, expresso o meu profundo agradecimento aos meus orientadores, o Professor Doutor Manuel Duarte Rocha e o Professor Doutor Paulo Mota, por toda a disponibilidade, apoio e conselhos essenciais que acompanharam este trabalho.A vossa exigência e orientação foram determinantes para o meu crescimento académico e pessoal.Agradeço, igualmente, aos meus amigos e a todos os colegas que, direta ou indiretamente, partilharam este percurso académico comigo, proporcionando momentos de incentivo e partilha importantes para a conclusão desta etapa.Por último, manifesto a minha mais sincera gratidão à minha família e à minha namorada, pelo apoio incondicional, pela paciência e compreensão em momentos mais exigentes desta jornada.Sem o vosso suporte, a concretização desta dissertação não teria sido possível.This dissertation aims to analyse the extent to which the introduction of the Digital Euro may influence the effectiveness of monetary policy transmission channels in the Euro Area, based on a critical review of the literature on CBDCs and an analysis of monetary transmission mechanisms.The analysis demonstrates that the macroeconomic impacts of a CBDC depend on its operational characteristics and on the structure of the financial system in which it is introduced.In this context, the analysis focuses on the Euro Area, where the Digital Euro will be implemented and where bank intermediation plays a particularly important role in the financial system.Theoretically, a universally accessible and interestbearing CBDC could strengthen monetary transmission.However, caution is required regarding the model adopted, as a CBDC, by exerting competitive pressure on deposits, may not only strengthen monetary policy transmission but also give rise to risks of banking disintermediation through the substitution of bank deposits with CBDC holdings, consequently creating risks to financial stability.Accordingly, the analysis of the Digital Euro considers the design features currently envisaged by the ECB, with particular emphasis on the non-remunerated CBDC, the holding limits for individual users, and the implementation of the waterfall mechanism, which connects the CBDC wallet to the user's bank account held with a commercial bank.Thus, when analysing the impact of the Digital Euro on the monetary policy transmission mechanism, it is found, in general, that no significant change in monetary policy transmission channels is expected, as the ECB intends the Digital Euro to serve as a retail payment instrument complementary to physical cash, rather than as a monetary policy instrument or a store of value, thereby seeking to safeguard the integrity of traditional bank intermediation.

## 384. [Kelemahan Tipifikasi Delik Eksploitasi Kerentanan Logika Sistem dalam Kasus Peretasan Kripto di Indonesia](<https://journal.uii.ac.id/psha/article/view/53466>)

- 作者：Amirudin Nur Wahid
- 发表日期：2026-11-16
- 引用次数：0
- [打开 PDF](<https://journal.uii.ac.id/psha/article/download/53466/21211>)

**摘要：**

The practice of exploiting system logic vulnerabilities on crypto trading platforms in the case of Suspect HS in Bandung in November 2025 reveals the limitations of Indonesian substantive criminal law dogmatics in responding to contemporary cyber-financial crime modi operandi. The absence of computer manipulation-based fraud offenses in positive law prompted law enforcement officials to apply a multi-layered statutory construction in a cumulative-alternative manner, encompassing the Electronic Information and Transactions Law, theft offenses in the Criminal Code, and the Funds Transfer Law. The application of these surrogate norms triggers juridical tension against the principle of legality, particularly the principles of lex certa, lex stricta, and the prohibition of analogy in criminal law. This normative legal research employs a statutory approach and a case approach by analyzing primary and secondary legal materials. The results of the research demonstrate two primary findings. First, there are juridical qualification errors and material norm disharmonies, wherein the forced application of fund transfer offenses and conventional theft to system flaw-based fictitious balance exploitation constitutes a form of analogy that contravenes the principle of nullum crimen, nulla poena sine lege stricta. Second, this research provides the formulaic construction of an electronic financial system manipulation fraud offense based on the substantive legality principle as a basis for written criminalization. The formulation emphasizes the act of manipulating data integrity or the processing logic of electronic systems to obtain unlawful economic benefits, so as to guarantee legal certainty without relying on expansive interpretations that violate the defendant's human rights. Keywords: Material Legality Principle, Market Manipulation, Crypto Assets, Norm Construction.

---

## 385. [Design of a Secure and Efficient Electronic Payment System Tailored for Mobile Users](<https://doi.org/10.67440/ahj.vi.3334>)

- 作者：Neeraj Kumar; Prerna Mohit
- 发表日期：2026-11-16
- 引用次数：0
- DOI：https://doi.org/10.67440/ahj.vi.3334
- [打开 PDF](<https://adolescenciaesaude.com/index.php/aes/article/download/3334/2515>)

**摘要：**

The objective of the program is to create a new electronic payment scheme that is secure and efficient and appropriate for mobile phone subscribers. In the area of mobile payments, the framework addresses important issues such as security, efficiency, user privacy, and lightweight processing appropriate for devices with restricted resources. A multi-level security approach is followed by our protocol, which consists of temporary identities, RSA digital signatures, and symmetric key cryptography. All this is achieved while maintaining a reasonable level of communication overhead. Through the establishment of a key management system that has been carefully designed, the system proposed establishes secure paths between the client, the merchant, the payment gateway, the issuer, and ultimately the acquirer. By shortening transaction processing times by 37% and computing overhead by 42%on mobile devices, our solution provides better performance compared to existing frameworks, as our experimental results demonstrate. This is achieved while maintaining the security requirements that are mandated by the National Institute of Standards and Technology (NIST). With significant gains in transaction throughput, security guarantee, and resource usage, the implementation results indicate that the system is acceptable for deployment in real-world settings.

## 386. [QShieldChain: A Quantum-Resistant Blockchain Framework Using CRYSTALS-Dilithium3 and AES for Secure Decentralized Transactions](<https://doi.org/10.47392/irjaeh.2026.0748>)

- 作者：U. Sudheer; P. Shyamala Madhuri
- 发表日期：2026-11-16
- 引用次数：0
- DOI：https://doi.org/10.47392/irjaeh.2026.0748
- [打开 PDF](<https://irjaeh.com/index.php/journal/article/download/2076/1909>)

**摘要：**

The security of traditional blockchain systems has been undermined by quantum computing through the following scenarios: – Traditional cryptographic algorithms like RSA and Elliptic Curve Cryptography (ECC) are vulnerable to quantum attacks. To mitigate these important vulnerabilities, this work proposes a secure and private blockchain system that utilizes two cryptographic methods: the CRYSTALS-Dilithium post-quantum digital signature and Advanced Encryption Standard (AES) symmetric encryption. The system proposed here improves the security of transactions in the system: post-quantum cryptography is used for verifying transactions, and AES encryption is used to keep the data in the transaction confidential. The proposed extension would encrypt all transaction information before storage, unlike existing blockchain systems that store the information in plain text, which would make it impossible for any unauthorized parties to access the information. It provides user registration, transaction generation, validation and storage on the blockchain via Ethereum smart contracts. The reason for the choice is that Dilithium3 is more secure, and has optimized latency. Experimental analysis shows that the proposed model provides high security, reliable transaction verification and better security from quantum computing attacks with acceptable performance. Comparative analysis shows that Dilithium3 offers a better key-size to latency tradeoff than both RSA and ECC, and has greater security guarantees. This study suggests a two-layer security model comprising AES encryption and CRYSTALS-Dilithium3 digital signatures in the context of Ethereum based blockchain environment, as a novel approach to secure decentralized transaction systems in post-quantum era. The framework is validated using a functional simulation environment that can be quantitatively evaluated.

---

## 387. [Global Macroeconomic Factors and The Net Asset Value of a USD-Denominated Sharia Equity Fund Focused on Greater China: A VAR Analysis](<https://doi.org/10.17358/ijf.1.2.123>)

- 作者：Arrasyid Putra Rafi; Deni Lubis
